## 🔴 s105 ADDENDUM — **THE RENDER DEPLOY HAD BEEN BROKEN SINCE s104; NOTHING FROM s104 OR s105 WAS EVER LIVE.** Ken pasted the deploy log: `build.sh` → `manage.py seed_all` → `-> seed_backfill_returns` → `Build failed 😞`. `seed_all` DISCOVERS every command named `seed_*` and runs it **with no arguments against production**; s104's `seed_backfill_returns` is not a form seeder at all — it takes a required `folder` positional pointing at roster CSVs that exist only on Ken's machine. It raised, `build.sh` runs under `set -o errexit`, the build died at that line, and **THREE consecutive deploys were silently blocked (s104 · s105 · the s105 DOB follow-up) while every session close reported "shipped"**. Confirmed empirically, not assumed: the live SPA bundle at `prep.delviotax.com/assets/index-*.js` still contained s103's `Start Return` and the OLD `Rolled forward from` banner, and contained neither `Seeded for 2025 back-entry` nor `theme-forest`. **THE FIX + THE CONTRACT:** a `seed_*` command runs on EVERY production deploy, so it must be (a) idempotent, (b) runnable with NO arguments, and (c) dependent on nothing outside the repo and the DB — otherwise it is an OPERATOR TOOL and declares `deploy_safe = False` on its `Command` class, which `seed_all` now skips (loudly). Auto-discovery of new FORM seeders is preserved deliberately. The guard is a **TEST, not a code review**: NEW `tests/test_seed_all_deploy_safety.py` **5/5** fails CI — where it is cheap — when a deploy-safe `seed_*` requires a positional, which is the exact shape that broke it. **ALSO NOTE FOR FUTURE SESSIONS: `/api/v1/version/` does NOT distinguish commits** (the VERSION file rarely changes) — verify a deploy landed by grepping the prod SPA bundle for a string unique to the build. **"Pushed" ≠ "deployed."** ▶ IMMEDIATE: watch the next Render deploy to GREEN, then confirm the bundle contains `theme-forest`. (The 3,279 back-entry shells are unaffected — the seeder was run locally against the prod DB, never via a deploy.)

The list was reproduced by QA on 2026-07-28 **on the LEGACY screens, three days before the cutover**, which is the single most important fact about it. **`8c0ddd4` QA #5** — a residential rental "fully depreciated" 13 years early. THREE real defects: the CSV importer defaulted a blank convention off the **METHOD CODE ALONE**, so a conversion rental written as plain `SL` (Lacerte and TaxWise both do) became 27.5-yr SL/HY and — there being no published fractional half-year table — computed **NO depreciation at all**; the **Lacerte parser's life group was `(\d+)`**, which cannot match "27.5", so **every rental line in a Lacerte conversion** imported with no method/convention/life (NOT reported by QA — found by sweeping the family after fixing the first); and the badge could not tell "depreciated out" from "no published table", so it printed reassurance over a live `D_4562_METHOD` error. §168(d)(2) + §168(c) verified against the statute. 23 tests, all three revert-proven. **`f668f69` NEW `D_4562_CONVENTION` + QA #4** — the new rule asks whether a combination is **LEGAL**, the question `D_4562_METHOD` ("can the engine compute it?") never asked: 27.5+HY fails LOUDLY, **39+HY computes 0.0256 instead of 0.02564 and NOTHING objects**. ⚠ **NO RS SPEC ENTRY EXISTS** (4562 spec R003 takes `convention` as a given; none of D001–D014 polices it) — written against the statute, queued for RS. **QA #4's prescribed fix was already the behaviour**: `compute_schedule_e` buckets a non-active rental to Part V and §469(i) only draws on bucket IV, returning the QA's own stated $0 allowed / $4,858 suspended. **The real defect: ALL SIX rental mutation paths called the entity-only K-2 rollup and NONE called `_recompute_1040`**, which returns early on a 1040 — every rental edit saved its row and recomputed nothing. Fixed at the shared helper (`_rental_mutated`), named for the EVENT because the old name described one of its two steps, which is how six call sites looked complete while doing half the work. **`c79249e` QA #8 + #9** — AGI ran through the `Math.abs` formatter (correct for refund/due, wrong for AGI). **#9 was a COMPUTE defect, not a diagnostic one**: with negative AGI the §170(b)(1) ceilings were multiplied straight through, so **Schedule A line 14 came out at −$3,223 with ZERO contributions**, inventing the carryover that fired `D_SCHA_004` — the prescription (gate the diagnostic) would have silenced the messenger and left a negative deduction on the return. Base floored at zero; positive-AGI math pinned unchanged. **`5e67ed2` QA #10** — adding a Schedule C POSTed the literal `"New Business"` into **line C, a tax-form field** that prints and transmits; every display site already had a fallback. **`7bd00d9` THE WORKFLOW UNBLOCK** — read-only count: **ALL 3,836 PRODUCTION RETURNS WERE `draft`**; the status field, API and both side effects existed but **nothing in the app could change a status**. ClientHeader pill → select. **Ken's ruling: preparers set status BY HAND during testing (the agent marks a return `filed` once it ties to the return as actually filed); autonomous transitions come with production.** ⚠⚠ NEW **`LEDGER_AUTOPOST_ENABLED` (default OFF, checked BEFORE reachability)** — `is_configured()` alone was NOT a guard: it keys on the credentials, so setting them to test the Ledger integration would have started invoicing **live clients from a test pass**, and testing runs on real already-filed returns. Live-proven: `filed` → baseline captured, outbox 0; `approved` → outbox STILL 0. ⚠⚠ **TWO ITEMS NO LONGER REPRODUCE AT ALL** — QA #1 (payer drafts) and QA #2 (1099-DIV reverting to zero) both passed their own acceptance tests on Slate; only a **stale totals strip** remains (display-only). **⚠⚠⚠ THE SESSION'S LESSON, TWICE OVER: (a) REPRODUCE BEFORE BUILDING — 3 of 6 prescriptions were already the behaviour or aimed at the wrong layer, and 2 more items were gone; (b) THE REVERT IS THE ONLY PROOF — FOUR times a GREEN test passed against a REVERTED fix** (a trip-wire matching its own docstring; a `not.toContain` matching its own comment; two overlapping mechanisms where a defensive clamp did all the work and made the real fix untestable; `is None` true for two different reasons). Source trip-wires now strip docstrings/comments via `ast`. Gates: FA **521** = baseline · vitest **1569** · tsc **46** = baseline · demo DB restored byte-clean (892/892). ▶ NOW WORKING ON: **idle — s175 closed. Ken is bringing a NEW QA list from ChatGPT/Codex; start there.** ▶ NEXT (if no new list): **QA #6 — source-summary rows offer fields the server REFUSES.** Confirmed live and worse than filed: a summary 1099-INT renders **Box 1 and Box 3 fully enabled**, both in `FORBIDDEN_ON_SUMMARY`; typing into Box 1 → **PATCH 400** → **no error shown and the input keeps the value** (silent data loss). ⚠ The fix is half-built and UNWIRED: `FORBIDDEN_ON_SUMMARY` + `summaryConflicts` exist in `client/src/renderer/components/forms/SourceSummary.tsx` with **NO consumers**. Then the **stale totals strip** (Ken: "come back to it"). Ken's ② MeF batch (`build_irs5695` scoped and ready, then 1116, then item 12's migration → 8615) remains queued. **③ THE STACKED DEPLOY SHIPPED at the s175 close (Ken's go)**: `main` → `fdbd7f2`, bundle `index-BrbsO-k6.js` verified carrying the status control, **`seed_rules` run on BOTH DBs (771 → 772 active rules, zero unresolvable rule_functions) — the seed debt is CLEARED**, post-deploy diagnostics probe clean. ⚠ **The carried "0227 un-applied on PROD" note was STALE** — `showmigrations` reported zero unapplied (an earlier deploy's `migrate --noinput` took it); the deploy was code-only. Remaining Ken items: ② and ④ the year-constant ruling.

## 🟢 **s174b (2026-08-01), Ken's item ②: `build_schedule1a` SHIPPED — THE OBBBA SCHEDULE FINALLY TRANSMITS (LEG 3 "missing MeF documents", 1 of 4)** (`676b6d8`, `slate-ui` + `main`, pushed, NO deploy — no migration, no seeded-rule change). 1040 line 13b sent `TotalAdditionalDeductionsAmt` with **no supporting schedule**, so every return claiming the OBBBA tips / overtime / car-loan-interest / enhanced-senior deductions was **paper-only**; NEW `build_schedule1a` emits `IRS1040Schedule1A` at its ReturnData1040.xsd sequence position (885, between Schedule 8812 and Schedule A). Element names AND line keys taken VERBATIM from the XSD's own `<LineNumber>` annotations — the app's seeded lines already matched one for one. ⚠⚠ **THE ATTACH GATE IS LINE 38, NOT the generic `_any(...)` every sibling schedule uses**: `compute_sch_1a` writes Part I's MAGI and the filing-status threshold on EVERY return that has a taxpayer, so `_any` is nonzero on essentially every computed 1040 and would have attached an **empty Schedule 1-A to all of them** (revert-proven). ⚠ Lines 8/16/25/31 (printed statutory maximums) have NO schema element — dropped deliberately and pinned. ⚠ The line-22 vehicle rows are `CarLoanVehicle` records, not FormFieldValues, so they build as `QlfyPassengerVehicleLoanIntGrp` groups inserted anchored on every element that FOLLOWS them (correct even when line 23 is absent); the printed form's `[:2]` truncation is deliberately NOT reproduced, so the transmitted line 23 foots against the transmitted rows even though the paper copy does not. **Proven by LIVE XSD VALIDATION against the real gated 2025v5.3 schemas** (tips-only + car-loan-with-two-groups), revert-proven twice (a pure element-ORDER swap fails validation with "This element is not expected"; the `_any` gate attaches the empty schedule). ⚠ **BOTH stale screen notices rewritten in the SAME commit** (the s173 lesson, 6th occurrence): the "Schedule 1-A is not transmitted … **file on paper**" error would have sent preparers to paper for a reason that no longer exists, and the vehicle-overflow note's bare "file on paper" is now the WORSE option since the XML carries every row — scoped to paper filing only; both vitest contracts rewritten carrying what they used to assert. Gates: 11 new tests · FA 521 · efile/mef/sch1a sweep 610 · vitest 1558 · tsc 46. ⚠⚠ **THE BATCH IS NOT "ONE PASS" — RE-SCOPE IT.** Verifying each form's REQUIRED elements against its XSD (do this before starting each one) found: **`build_irs8615` is BLOCKED on a migration** — `IRS8615.xsd` makes `ParentNm`, `ParentNameControlTxt`, the parent's `SSN` and `IndividualReturnFilingStatusCd` ALL REQUIRED at top level, and the model has `parent_filing_status` but **no `parent_name` and no `parent_ssn`**, so **LEG 3 item 11 DEPENDS ON item 12** (which the queue lists separately, and which is a migration → stage-then-Ken-deploys). **`build_irs5695` is NOT blocked** (only `NameLine1Txt` + `SSN` are top-level required, both already in the header; the §25C door/window detail is required only inside its optional groups) — **do 5695 next**. **`build_irs1116` is not blocked but is the biggest** (15 required incl. the income-CATEGORY indicators a–g and the per-country column group; emit ONLY on path "full"). ⚠ **PREMISE CORRECTION — `IRS8615` (1630) and `IRS1116` (1067) ARE declared in ReturnData1040.xsd** in 2025v5.3, v5.4 AND 2026v1.0; the s147/s148 write-ups assert they are not, and were wrong. **Nothing here is blocked by an IRS schema limitation** — only 8615 by our own missing columns. 3rd and 4th queued premise to fail verification (after backlog items 3 and 6). ▶ NOW WORKING ON: **idle — s174 closed.** ▶ NEXT: **`build_irs5695` — SCOPED, READY TO BUILD** (see the tax-app STATUS.md resume pointer for the full scoping). Headlines: the document is TWO independent optional top-level groups (§25D `ResidentialCleanEnergyCrGrp` lines 1–16, §25C `EgyEffcntHmImprvCrGrp` 17a–32b), and every "required" element is required only INSIDE an optional group — including the item sub-groups — so omitting a sub-group drops its requirements (a flat required-element grep says 29 and badly overstates it; check NESTING DEPTH). ⚠⚠ **The data is on `Taxpayer.e5695_*`, NOT in the FFV rows** — the seeded FORM_5695 rows are a LIGHT FACE with aggregate keys (`l1_5` = lines 1–5b combined), so `_form_scoped_lines` alone cannot produce per-line 1/2/3/4; the per-item costs, the tri-state gates and the **QM ID PINs** (which §25C(h) makes a CONDITION of the credit) are all model fields. ⚠ `render_5695` (renderer.py:11656, the v2 whole-face version) ALREADY derives numeric lines from those same fields — do NOT re-derive blind (the s150/s173 8960 drift trap); the XML's structure differs (nested groups vs a flat line map) so it cannot consume the render face directly, but the field→line mapping is shared and must be **PINNED BY AN AGREEMENT TEST** with `render_5695` (the s173 precedent) rather than by refactoring the verified renderer. ⚠ Tri-state gates are `None = not reached` — **omit the indicator, never send a false**; a No forfeits the credit. Then `build_irs1116`, then item 12's migration → `build_irs8615`. Then Ken's ③ the stacked deploy (**migrate 0227 on PROD + `seed_rules` BOTH DBs, s169–s172**) and ④ the year-constant ruling.

## 🟢 **s174 (2026-08-01): THE SLATE CUTOVER — `NEW_UI` NOW DEFAULTS ON** (`590b173`, `slate-ui` + `main` fast-forwarded, pushed, NO deploy — no migration, no seeded-rule change, server untouched). **Ken set the order for what follows LEG 2: ① flip the flag → ② the MeF documents batch → ③ the stacked deploy → ④ the year-constant ruling.** ① is DONE. **The polarity flipped, it was not removed:** default ON everywhere; **`VITE_NEW_UI=0` opts a BUILD out and is ABSOLUTE** (it beats the per-browser override, because it is the rollback lever for a bad deploy — one Render env var, no code change, no revert; and **nothing in `build.sh` sets the var today**, so the next deploy produces a Slate-default bundle on its own); `localStorage["delvio-new-ui"]="0"` drops ONE browser back to legacy so a preparer who hits a Slate problem mid-return can finish that return on the old screen. ⚠ **Legacy is now the FALLBACK, not dead code** — the override is a runtime read, so both branches ship; accepted, the lever is worth the bundle. ⚠⚠ **THE SESSION'S LESSON: WHEN A FLAG FLIPS, ~160 TESTS SILENTLY CHANGE BRANCH.** The flip turned **162 tests across 17 files red**, every one a LEGACY-container suite that had merely started rendering the Slate branch — verified, not assumed (the s152 envelope test's REAL assertion passes under Slate; only its legacy wording fails). Rewriting them to Slate would not have re-pointed that coverage, it would have **DELETED** it at exactly the moment the fallback became the thing you fall back TO — so `test/setup.ts` seeds the override "0" suite-wide and the legacy pins stand. **This is the one place the test default and the app default disagree on purpose**; both files say so and `featureFlags.test.ts` pins the arrangement itself (revert-proven: removing the seed fails the guard AND breaks the container tests). **THE STRAGGLERS WERE VERIFIED, NOT ASSUMED:** the 1041 editor and the state-return editor interiors are unconverted and now render legacy bodies inside the Slate chrome; they pass `groups={undefined}` so `LeftRail` falls back to a flat "Sections" list of every tab — **live-proven on the 1041 (all 9 tabs render, legacy body loads)**. ⚠ The state interiors share that exact branch but were **NOT independently live-run**: the only state returns in the shared DB belong to **The Tax Shelter's real clients**, and the demo/dev firms have none. Also closed the **s162 nav-coverage gap on the 1040** — the entity navs have been pinned since unit 42, the 1040 never was because a dropped tab was still reachable on the legacy tab row; the flip removed that net (clean today, revert-proven with an orphan tab). ⚠ **HASH ROUTER:** deep links are `#/tax-returns/<id>/editor`; a path-style URL silently renders the root route, and setting `location.href` to a `#/...` URL is a SAME-DOCUMENT navigation so the module never re-evaluates — judge a flag change by a real reload, and judge login by the firm name on screen. Gates: vitest **1558** (1556 + 2 new pins) · tsc **46** = baseline · production build green. ▶ NOW WORKING ON: *(superseded — see the s174b entry above: `build_schedule1a` shipped and the batch was re-scoped)* ▶ NEXT: **Ken's item ② — the MeF documents batch** (`build_schedule1a` / `build_irs8615` / `build_irs1116` / `build_irs5695`; all four confirmed ABSENT from `composition/mappers/y2025/builder.py` and all four XSDs confirmed LOCAL at `docs/mef/schemas/2025v5.3/2025v5.3/IndividualIncomeTax/Common/` — 543/337/885/1,055 lines; per form a source dataclass + extract + builder + a slot at XSD sequence position in `build_return` + the manifest + tests). Then ③ the stacked deploy (**migrate 0227 on PROD + `seed_rules` BOTH DBs, s169–s172**) and ④ the year-constant ruling.

## 🟢 **s170 (2026-07-31), backlog LEG 2 item 6: THE FORM 8863 LINE-7 LOCKOUT — RESOLVED BY VERIFICATION; THE PRESCRIBED FIX WAS WRONG AND WAS NOT BUILT** (`slate-ui`, pushed, NO deploy — ⚠ `seed_rules` on BOTH DBs at the next deploy: NEW rule `D_8863_LOCKOUT_NA` + the `D_8863_LOCKOUT` description; no migration). The item said "key it per student, not `any()`" — verified FIRST against the 2025 f8863 face ("If YOU were under age 24" — the FILER), §25A(i)(5) ("shall not apply to any TAXPAYER"), the RS spec's R-8863-AOTC-PHASEOUT and the MeF IRS8863.xsd's single return-level RefundableAmerOppCrUnder24Ind: the lockout is RETURN-WIDE by law, the engine's `any()` was already correct, and a per-student split would have been a change of law. ## 🟢 **s148 (2026-07-30), unit 28: FORM 1116 / FOREIGN TAX CREDIT SHIPPED (`19e92b1`, `slate-ui`, pushed, no deploy).** **32 of ~39 screens; the sweep resumes at Form 8880.** The s132 **SINGLETON** — screenbar + InputRow worksheets over Form1116Section's TWO PATCH lanes (the singleton form row + the return-level de minimis opt-out on Taxpayer). ⚠ **NO SERVER CHANGE** — `compute_1116_db` already publishes the whole 31-row FORM_1116 face to FormFieldValue, and `field_values` already ships every form's rows to the client, so the screen renders the engine's face as locked ƒx cells scoped by `form_code` (ratios 3f/19 display the 4-dp string raw). Engine constants re-verified against the LIVE i1116: the $20,000 QD/gain ceiling and the $394,600 MFJ-QSS / $197,300 adjustment-exception thresholds. ⭐ **FINDINGS — one presentation fix shipped, two legs exposed, one latent shape re-found.** **(1) ✅ FIXED — THE §904(j) CEILING COPY IGNORED QSS**: the legacy screen keyed $600 off `filing_status === "mfj"` alone while `elect_ceiling` and §904(j)(3)(B) give a qualifying surviving spouse $600 too; a QSS filer read "$300" on every explainer. Revert-tested — restoring the MFJ-only line failed exactly the QSS test. **(2) ⚠⚠ E-FILE — FORM 1116 IS NEVER TRANSMITTED BUT THE FULL-PATH CREDIT IS.** No `IRS1116` builder while Schedule 3 line 1 maps to `ForeignTaxCreditAmt`. Correct on the §904(j) paths (the statute's point is that no 1116 is filed) — but a FULL-limitation return transmits the credit with no form behind it. **4th occurrence of the missing-document shape (SCH_1A, 8615 ×2) — scope as ONE "missing MeF documents" work item**, and the 1116 builder must emit ONLY on path "full". → LEG 3. **(3) ⚠ RENDER — THE PRINTED PART II IS INCOMPLETE IN THREE INSTRUCTION-BACKED WAYS**: the face's mandatory (j) Paid / (k) Accrued box is never checked (i1116: cash-basis default is Paid), column (l) prints blank where i1116 directs **"Enter '1099 taxes' in column (l)"** for 1099-reported tax, only the (u) total is filled with the (q)–(t) withheld-at-source breakdown empty, and header box h "Resident of" is blank. Render-only except a possible box-h field. → LEG 3. **(4) ⚠ LATENT — `ADJ_EXCEPTION_TI.get(year) or [2026]`**, pinned 2025/2026, and `D_1116_007` covers ONLY year==2026 — an unpinned 2027 return would silently test against the 2026 figures. **FOURTH find of the year-fallback shape** → strengthens LEG 4 item 17 (sweep the engine, rule once), not a new item. The screen names the year. ⚠⚠ **THE LIVE RUN CAUGHT A BUG THE SUITE COULD NOT (a prop-based screen this time)**: SCH_3 line 1 is seeded **`is_computed=False`** (the manual-FTC escape hatch), so the auto-credit pill keyed off `is_computed` read "Not engaged" with $250 sitting on the line. **A seeded line's `is_computed` describes the LINE, not the value's author — engine-written = `!is_overridden`** (every manual save sets it, views.py:2678). ## 🟢 **s147 (2026-07-30), unit 27: FORM 8615 / KIDDIE TAX SHIPPED (`1c0fcc1`, `slate-ui`, pushed, no deploy).** **31 of ~39 screens; the sweep resumes at Form 1116.** The s132 **SINGLETON** — screenbar + InputRow worksheets; structurally the Schedule J screen's twin (one row per child return, and the computed line 18 REPLACES 1040 line 16). ⚠ **NO SERVER CHANGE, unlike unit 26** — `compute_8615_db` already persists the whole face onto the Form8615 row, which arrives as a prop, so nothing needed an endpoint and nothing on the screen computes tax. One is presentation and is FIXED; three are stated at the cell that causes them.** **(1) ✅ FIXED — THE SCREEN HAD BEEN TELLING PREPARERS A LIE SINCE JUNE.** It said qualified dividends / net capital gain "are not yet computed (each defers to manual preparation)" and carried `QDCGT_PENDING` copy — but the cap-gain path was BUILT 2026-06-24 (`compute_8615._line5_split` = the i8615 Line 5 Worksheets 1/2/3, plus the QDCGT reuse on lines 9/15/17), `_red_defer_code` can no longer return that code, and `d_8615_008` was RETIRED the same day. Engine-proven: 6,000 QD + 4,000 net cap gain returns `red_defer_code == ""` and a line 18 of 3,065. **A preparer following the stale banner would have worked by hand a form the software had already figured** — second occurrence of the s136 SS-lump-sum shape. Corrected in the legacy screen too. **(2) ⚠⚠ ENGINE — LINE 1 COUNTS ONLY THREE KINDS OF UNEARNED INCOME AND IS READ-ONLY.** `_source_child_amounts` = 1040 2b + 3b + max(0, 7). **i8615, fetched live from irs.gov**, defines unearned income as *"generally all income other than salaries, wages, and other amounts received as pay for work actually performed"* and lists rents, royalties, pensions and annuities, taxable scholarships, unemployment, alimony, the taxable part of social security and **"income (other than earned income) received as the beneficiary of a trust"** — and directs a child with NO earned income to enter **ADJUSTED GROSS INCOME**. Engine-proven: a child with 18,000 of trust income (AGI 20,000) gets line 18 = **2,003 instead of 3,770, 1,767 UNDERSTATED**; a 14,000 survivor annuity gives **1,343 instead of 2,560, 1,217 UNDERSTATED**. A minor who is a trust beneficiary is exactly what §1(g) exists to reach, and `child_unearned_income` is `read_only_fields`, so there is **no override to escape with**. → LEG 2. **(3) ⚠ RENDER — THE PRINTED FORM'S WHOLE PARENT HEADER IS BLANK.** `f8615_2025.py` maps neither box A (parent's name) nor box B (parent's SSN), and box C (filing status, `c1_1[0..4]`) is dismissed as "metadata" though `parent_filing_status` IS stored and drives the line-9 rate schedule. The face itself prints "Caution: See instructions before completing" beside box A. Box C is render-only; A/B need a migration. → LEG 3. **(4) ⚠⚠ E-FILE — FORM 8615 IS NEVER TRANSMITTED BUT THE TAX IT PRODUCES IS.** No `IRS8615` builder anywhere in `apps/efile/` and no IRS8615 element in the ReturnData1040.xsd sequence (its siblings IRS2441 / IRS6251 / IRS5329 / IRS8880 all have one), while line 18 overwrites 1040 line 16 and line 16 maps to `TaxAmt`. **The s140 Schedule 1-A finding's SECOND OCCURRENCE — scope the two together.** A kiddie-tax return is paper-only. → LEG 3. ⚠ **LATENT (reported as latent, NOT claimed as live): the year-constant fallback.** `line2_amount_for`/`std_floor_for` are `TABLE.get(year) or TABLE[_LATEST_YEAR]`, pinned 2025/2026 only — but both years carry the same 1,350 / 2,700, so **no dollars move today**. Third occurrence of the shape (s142 `D_SR_UNPINNED_YEAR`, s146's four AMT tables); the recommendation is now to **sweep the engine and rule once** rather than find it a fourth time. ⚠⚠ **"UNIT FULLY COMPLETE" ON A TRACKER ROW IS A CLAIM, NOT A FACT** — Form 8615's row carried all six legs ✅ while having no e-file leg at all and an incomplete render leg. **Second form to prove the missing-columns item** (SCH_1A was the first); the row is now annotated. ⚠ **The live run still earned its keep on a screen that fetches nothing**: with line 18 equal to line 17 the summary read "$17,425 against $17,425" — true and useless, and that is the §1(g)-FLOOR case where the preparer most needs telling that §1(g) added nothing. Rewritten and tested. ⚠ **`slate_screen_screenshots.mjs` now ESCAPES the rail label** before it becomes a `RegExp` — "Kiddie Tax (8615)" was silently a capture group matching "Kiddie Tax 8615", dying as a bare 30s TimeoutError; pass the label VERBATIM from `IndividualNav.tsx` now. **Gates:** new `slateForm8615Screen.test.tsx` **18** (revert-tested — restoring the QDCGT lie failed exactly the 2 tests that pin it) · the five 8615 server legs **63** · flow assertions **521** (baseline exactly) · vitest **1157/1157** (the real pre-unit baseline is **1139**, not the 1129 s146 recorded — measured by re-running with the new file excluded) · tsc **46 = baseline**. **LIVE-PROVEN** on the demo QA return with a synthetic kiddie-tax scenario (line 1 = 25,950 → line 5 = 23,250 → line 9 = 27,896 → line 18 = 17,425 → 1040 line 16 = 17,425), every on-screen ƒx cell matching the ORM; **writes REVERTED with ZERO drift, ORM-verified 892 of 892 FormFieldValue rows identical**. ## 🟢 **s146 (2026-07-30), unit 26: FORM 6251 / ALTERNATIVE MINIMUM TAX SHIPPED (`1436cf1`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** **THE SWEEP IS BACK ON** — Ken: *"Let's get back to the redesign unless this is pressing."* Backlog item 5 is not pressing (nothing is in front of clients until Jan 2027 and the Sch 1-A screen already flags it), so LEG 2 PAUSES at item 5. **30 of ~39 screens; the sweep resumes at Form 8615.** The s132 **SINGLETON** — screenbar + InputRow worksheets; one form per return, every input an `amt_*` Taxpayer fact, so no DocumentTabs and no PayerTable. ⚠⚠ **ONE ADDITIVE READ-ONLY SERVER CHANGE**: `compute_6251_face` always produced the whole face and is the single source render + diagnostics read, but NOTHING published it (`compute_6251_db` persists only Sch 2 line 2), so the legacy screen showed the AMT and nothing behind it. New endpoint `GET /tax-returns/<id>/form-6251-face/` in **`apps/returns/amt_face.py` — kept OUT of views.py** because that file carries another session's in-flight work (the `rm_aggregates.py` precedent, which its own docstring already establishes). ⚠⚠ **TWO ENGINE DEFECTS, engine-proven, NOT fixed (a sweep unit is presentation-only) and now surfaced on the screen:** (1) **an explicit 0 cannot override an add-back** — `_override_or` is `v if v != ZERO else default` and all five fields are `DecimalField(default=0)` non-nullable, so zero is inexpressible AND an untouched cell arrives as "0.00", identical to a typed 0; **8,400 OVERSTATED** (std-deduction add-back) and **1,400 UNDERSTATED** (line-2b refund) on an AMT-biting return; fix = nullable fields = a migration = LEG 3. (2) **unpinned tax years silently borrow another year's constants** — `table.get(year) or table[_FALLBACK_YEAR]` across four tables with only 2025/2026 pinned; the identical return gives **28,169 for TY2025 and 46,310 for TY2027**, and none of the eight D_6251_* diagnostics says so (the s142 `D_SR_UNPINNED_YEAR` shape). ⚠⚠ **TWO BUGS IN MY OWN WORK, BOTH FOUND ONLY BY THE LIVE RUN** and neither reachable from a unit test (the tests pass the face in as a prop): the client never unwrapped the server's `{"data": ...}` envelope, and `face === undefined` (loading) was collapsed into `ready:false` — so a perfectly computable return read **"Form 6251 does not apply to this return"**, for long enough to screenshot.
