## 🟢 **s145 (2026-07-30): KEN'S BACKLOG — LEG 2 ITEM 4 COMPLETE (`3850e2d` on `slate-ui`, NO DEPLOY).** **KEN RULED on the education-credit election, and the item is built.** §25A(c)(2)(A) forbids one student taking BOTH education credits, but it is an **ELECTION** — the LLC is barred only *"if the taxpayer **elects** to have [the AOTC] apply with respect to such individual"* — so "drop the LLC wherever the AOTC is allowed" would impose an election nobody made. **Ken's ruling: a nonzero AOTC amount on an AOTC-eligible student IS the election** (`compute_8863.aotc_elected`), and that student leaves the line-10 LLC base. Entering $0 AOTC is how a preparer chooses LLC-only; an ineligible student cannot elect at all, so their LLC expenses stand. **The same $4,000 can no longer earn both credits — $800 on the s138 proof return.** Ken considered and rejected an explicit per-student election field (a migration + one more box) and leaving compute alone behind the blocking error. Ruling recorded in **DECISIONS.md**. ⚠⚠ **`D_8863_DUAL_STUDENT` DEMOTED error → warning** — s142 promoted it only because compute did not enforce the rule; it does now, so nothing is left to block, and `warning` is the severity the RS spec carries. The message names the student, the dollars on BOTH sides and which credit the student was put on, so the election is never silent. ⚠⚠ **The app is now knowingly AHEAD of a SECOND spec** — `R-8863-LLC` sums every student's LLC expenses with no §25A(c)(2)(A) exclusion, and the spec KNOWS the rule (it carries a warning diagnostic) but never as a COMPUTATION. Fetched live (key `FORM_8863`) and diffed against the cached copy — identical but for the export timestamp. Flagged in REVIEW_QUEUE **with the PATTERN named: three forms (5329, 8863, Schedule 1-A) now diverge in the same direction, their specs carrying the arithmetic but not the statutory exception — one conversation, not three tickets.** ⚠⚠ **THE FIRST DRAFT OF THE NEW TEST SUITE COULD NOT SEE ITS OWN REGRESSION** — its helper re-derived the two bases with `aotc_elected` instead of reading what `compute_8863_db` wrote, so reverting the fix left every test in the file passing. It now runs a real `compute_return` and reads FORM_8863 lines 1 and 10. **A test that re-implements the thing it tests is blind by construction.** Both fixes revert-tested (4 and 3 failures). Gates: new suite **12** · flow assertions **521** (baseline exactly) · 8863 / Topic 8 / Sch 3 / MeF-1040 bands **298**. No migration; `seed_rules` on BOTH DBs at deploy (a SEVERITY change — it lives in two places, the s109b lesson). ▶ NEXT: **LEG 2 item 5** — the Schedule 1-A tips owner filter. *(PAUSED in s146 — Ken redirected to the redesign.)*

## 🟢 **s144 (2026-07-30): KEN'S BACKLOG — LEG 2 ITEM 3 COMPLETE (`0800455` on `slate-ui`, NO DEPLOY).** **Form 5329 Part I line 4 is now a genuine 25%/10% BLEND.** The 25% rides the code-S (SIMPLE first-2-years) slice of line 3 and 10% the rest, per the 2025 Instructions for Form 5329 heading "Line 4" (Cat. No. 13330R — *"you must multiply **that amount** by 25% instead of 10%… designated with code S in box 7"*; §72(t)(6)). The s141 proof case falls from **13,000 to 5,500** and the worse one from 25,125 to 10,125. **17 new tests; all three fixes revert-tested (10 / 4 / 1 failures); LIVE-PROVEN on the demo QA return and reverted with ZERO drift, ORM-verified 892/892 FormFieldValue rows; no migration.** ⚠⚠ **The app is now knowingly AHEAD of `R-5329-02`**, which still carries the whole-line shortcut — the spec was fetched live and diffed against the cached copy (identical but for the export timestamp), so this is FLAGGED in REVIEW_QUEUE, not silently diverged. Second occurrence of the s143 `R-8863-LLC` shape. ⚠ **`or has_s` is GONE** — a code-S document now rates its OWN slice and the `simple_25pct` checkbox is the preparer's whole-line assertion, so both controls finally mean something (this also closed s141 defect 5). ⚠ **THE ONE JUDGMENT CALL: line 2 is apportioned PRO RATA** — the form carries one un-attributed exception figure and i5329 is silent; worth up to $750 on a 50k return against the alternatives, written up for Ken and pinned by a test named for it. ⚠ **`part_i_line4` is the ONE helper** — `compute_5329_full` AND `compute_retirement.compute_5329_part_i` (the pre-dual helper the FA-1040-5329 flow assertions exercise) both delegate, so the two engines cannot drift. ⚠ **`part_i_split` is a NEW read-only serializer field, deliberately NOT a `computed_lines` key** — the renderer and the MeF extract iterate that dict by LINE NUMBER and the IRS form has no line for the split. ⚠ **Seed change: the D_RET_007 description** — `seed_rules` on BOTH DBs at deploy (no migration). Gates: new suite **17** · flow assertions **521** (baseline exactly) · Topic 5 / 5329 / diagnostics **87** · retirement + render + e-file **220** (1 skipped) · vitest **1116/1116** · tsc **46 = baseline**. ▶ NEXT: **LEG 2 item 5** — the Schedule 1-A tips owner filter. *(Item 4 was unblocked and built the same day — see s145.)*

## 🟢 **s143 (2026-07-30): KEN'S BACKLOG — LEG 2 ITEMS 1 + 2 COMPLETE (`4c76624` on `slate-ui`, pushed, NO DEPLOY).** The first compute fixes of the backlog. **8 new tests, every fix revert-tested, no migration, no seed change, no client change** — compute-only, so it needs nothing at deploy beyond the push. ⭐ **(1) ITEM 1 — the stale QBI deduction on 1040 line 13 ($859 understated).** `compute_8995_db`'s not-engaged branch left line 13 alone on the stated grounds that "a direct entry survives", but line 13 is a COMPUTED line (`seed_1040`: `is_computed=True`), so a genuine preparer figure is an OVERRIDE and `write_line_13`'s `is_overridden` guard already protected it. What the old branch actually preserved was residue: delete the last §199A source and the deduction it produced stayed for ever, still reducing taxable income, and recomputing never cleared it. Now `blank_form_rows() + write_line_13("")`, which pops `values["13"]` so the 14/15 reflow recovers the taxable income in the same pass. **The fix FOLLOWS the RS spec rather than diverging** — `R-8995-L15` makes 1040 line 13a the Form 8995 line-15 figure, so with no line 15 the line is blank. ⚠⚠ **NEW FINDING, never previously logged: it was an E-FILE defect too.** 1040 line 13 maps to `QualifiedBusinessIncomeDedAmt` in `builder.py`'s `LINE_ORDER`, and the builder OMITS a blank line from the XML while emitting a stored one — so the stale figure was **transmitted** as a real QBI deduction with the Form 8995 rows blank, a deduction claimed with no supporting form behind it. Lesson banked: **follow a defective line into E-FILE, not just into print.** ⭐ **(2) ITEM 2 — the `disengage()`-guarded-on-`!= ZERO` family, and the ROOT CAUSE was narrower than the backlog assumed.** The guard was the symptom; the cause was that `compute_8863_db` wrote the nonrefundable credit to Schedule 3 line 3 **unconditionally** while the refundable half FIVE LINES UP already wrote `""` at zero — two writes in the same function disagreeing. So the one value the engaged path could produce (a credit phased out / MFS-barred / with no qualifying expenses stores a literal `"0"`) was the one value the clear-up refused to touch, surviving the deletion of every student. Fixed at BOTH ends. ⚠ **The instructed "sweep the family" was AUDITED, not assumed, and the honest answer is that the other three were LATENT, not defective** — `compute_1116` writes `""` when its credit is `<= 0`, and `compute_8960` / `compute_2210` call `disengage()` outright when their amounts are `<= 0`, so none could ever write the zero its guard refused to clear; reporting four live bugs would have been false. All three hardened anyway, with `test_sibling_modules_never_write_a_zero_feeder` **pinning the reason each is safe**. Every guard now detects the change through the write itself, so an already-blank row costs nothing and no longer fires a pointless `compute_sch_2/3` reflow. No dollar moves on item 2 — a blank and a "0" sum identically. 🔴 **ITEM 4 (one student taking BOTH education credits, $800) was DELIBERATELY NOT BUILT and is escalated to Ken.** The spec fetch (the CLAUDE.md gate) found that `R-8863-LLC` says outright *"L10 = Σ student LLC adjusted expenses"* with no §25A(c)(2)(A) exclusion — the spec carries the same defect, and only knows the rule as a *warning diagnostic*, never as a computation (the item-3 `R-5329-02` shape again). Worse, **§25A(c)(2)(A) is an ELECTION** — it bars the LLC only *"if the taxpayer elects"* the AOTC for that student — so the backlog's one-line framing would have compute silently pick one credit over another. Recommendation logged in `REVIEW_QUEUE.md`: treat nonzero `aotc_expenses` on an eligible student AS the election. **Gates:** new `tests/test_backlog_leg2_compute.py` **8** (incl. 3 negative controls + a positive control proving `D_8995_STALE`'s new silence is the compute fix and not a broken rule) · flow assertions **521** (baseline exactly) · Topic 8 / 8995 **166** · 8863 / 1116 **86** · 8960 / 2210 **119**. **All three fixes revert-tested** — reverting the line-13 write failed 2 tests, the 8863 write 1, the 8863 guard 2 (behaviour + source trip-wire). ⚠ A test-authoring trap worth keeping: the first item-1 test deleted the only Schedule C on a Schedule-C-only return, which takes the income with it and floors taxable income at zero, **hiding the very effect under test** — it now carries W-2 wages alongside. ⚠ The RS key for Form 8863 is `FORM_8863`, not `8863` (Form 8995's IS the bare `8995` — there is no single convention; use the app's `FormDefinition.code`). ▶ NEXT: **LEG 2 item 3** — the Form 5329 SIMPLE-rate blend. *(DONE in s144, `0800455`.)*

## 🟢 **s142 (2026-07-30): KEN'S BACKLOG — LEG 1 (DIAGNOSTICS ONLY) COMPLETE (`slate-ui`, no deploy).** Three commits: `2ed5eff` Schedule 1-A diagnostics, `42eb851` the two Form 5329 fixes, `d991b50` the s137 five + two severity promotions. **12 new diagnostics, 2 severity promotions, 1 dual-awareness rebuild, 71 new tests, no compute change, no migration, no renderer change.** ⭐ **(1) NEW `apps/diagnostics/rules_sch_1a.py` — Schedule 1-A had NO diagnostics at all**, and `compute_sch_1a`'s own docstring still deferred D_SCH1A_001..006 to "when the 1040 diagnostics framework exists", which it has for a long time. Implemented from the live SCH_1A spec (byte-identical to the cached `sch_1a_spec.json`) cross-checked against the 2025 form face: all six specced checks PLUS **`D_SCH1A_NO_DOB`**, which the spec has no entry for and which carries the money — a valid-SSN filer with no `date_of_birth` while line 35 > 0. `_born_before(None, cutoff)` returns False, so a blank date of birth is silently read as "not a senior": line 36a/36b prints $0 while line 35 keeps printing the amount forfeited. **Live-proven READ-ONLY on the demo QA return at rest** (its NULL date_of_birth is deliberate): line 35 = 4,826, line 37 = 0, the rule reports **$4,826** forfeited, the other six correctly silent, ZERO writes. ⚠⚠ **THE SPEC'S CONDITIONS CANNOT FIRE AS WRITTEN, AND THAT IS THE FINDING:** D_SCH1A_001/002 are specced as `filing_status == 'mfs' AND tips_deduction > 0` (and the valid-SSN equivalent), but compute already short-circuits Parts II/III/V to all zeros in exactly those states — the deduction is structurally incapable of exceeding zero, so read literally both rules are **dead code**. Every rule is therefore written against the **INPUTS**: what the preparer needs to be told is that the entries are being silently discarded. Severities unchanged. ⚠ **Two spec facts have no model field** — `tips_occupation_on_irs_list` is conflated with the §199A non-SSTB test in the single boolean `tips_eligible_for_deduction` (so D_SCH1A_003 is a superset, correct either way), and `tips_multiple_employers_or_occupations` is absent entirely, so D_SCH1A_006 **derives** the employer half from the tipped W-2s while the multiple-OCCUPATION half is not derivable and is not covered. ⚠ **D_SCH1A_004 is deliberately narrowed** to the 12-month near-miss band, because nothing in this app is an affirmative senior *claim* (Part V is derived from `date_of_birth`) and the literal condition would fire on every filer under 65 whose MAGI leaves line 35 above zero. ⚠ The line-4a **$176,100** threshold is the year's social security wage base and is **pinned per year (2025 only)** — unpinned years SKIP the rule rather than reuse a stale base (the s137 PY_STD_DEDUCTION lesson, applied prospectively). All four spec points → REVIEW_QUEUE for the next RS session; **never silently diverged.** **(2) The two Form 5329 fixes.** `D_5329_003` warning → **ERROR**: the conservative blank-value default STAYS (the engine must not guess an account value) but the preparer can no longer file past it, and the message now names the tax per account and states outright that **an explicit 0 is a DIFFERENT answer from a blank** — the distinction nothing on screen made ($180 with the value at 3,000, $0 with an explicit 0, $420 blank). `D_RET_007` **rebuilt DUAL-AWARE** through `_f5329_state`: it read the deprecated `Taxpayer.f5329_simple_25pct` scalar plus a return-wide code-S scan, so a preparer who ticked the per-owner box with no code-S document got the 25% rate **silently, from the one check built to catch exactly that**. It now reports per owner and names WHICH of the two independent causes engaged the rate — they are kept apart in the state dict *because compute ORs them*, which is precisely why an unticked box can sit on a return taxed at 25% — and it flags the still-open whole-line-3 overstatement. **(3) The s137 five + two severity corrections**, each proven against the engine's pure functions in a throwaway probe (no browser) and each naming its dollars: **`D_SR_5E_BLANK` (error)** a blank prior-year Schedule A 5e makes `worksheet_2a` read the entire 5d as capped away, so a $3,000 refund with 5d = 12,000 / 5e = 10,000 goes from **$1,000** taxable to **$0** — D_SR_INCOMPLETE checks 5d and line 17 but not 5e; **`D_W2G_LOSS_CAP` (error)** the §165(d) cap reads a preparer field, not the winnings the return reports (W-2G box 1 + `other_gambling_winnings`), so a blank/low cap makes `min(losses, 0)` = 0 and the whole deduction vanishes while a high cap allows losses above the ceiling — the rule reports which direction and how much; **`D_8889_FAMILY_ZERO` (error)** Form 8889 line 6 is a genuine THREE-state cap and an explicit 0 asserts the whole family limit went to the spouse, driving lines 8/12/13 and the deduction to $0 (the s137 $8,550 → $0 swing); **`D_SR_AGE_BLIND_COUNT` (warning)** `py_standard_deduction` multiplies the per-box amount by the count with **no ceiling** while Form 1040 line 12d has only four boxes (2024 single: 4 = 22,400, 5 = 24,350, 12 = 38,000), inflating the prior-year standard deduction and understating the taxable refund; **`D_SR_UNPINNED_YEAR` (warning)** `PY_STD_DEDUCTION.get(refund_year, PY_STD_DEDUCTION[2024])` is a FALLBACK not an error and only 2024/2025 are pinned, so a TY2027 return quietly uses 2024 — the same inputs gave **$500** taxable on 2025 constants and **$1,000** on the fallback; **`D_8863_DUAL_STUDENT` warning → ERROR** (§25A(c)(2)(A) forbids one student taking both credits, compute does not enforce it, the same $4,000 earned an extra $800 — the compute fix is LEG 2 and still open, which is why this must block); **`D_8995_STALE` (error, NEW, no spec entry)** a QBI deduction on 1040 line 13 with no §199A source — the s139 stale-deduction defect, $859 understated, unaffected by two recomputes; it fires only when line 13 > 0, is NOT overridden (`write_line_13` already protects a genuine direct entry) and `qbi_engaged` is False, so what is left can only be residue. **Live read-only check on the demo QA return at rest: all nine new or changed rules report ZERO findings** — no false positives, and **s142 wrote NOTHING to the demo data**. GATES: NEW `test_sch_1a_diagnostics_leg.py` **29/29** · NEW `test_5329_diagnostics_s142.py` **14/14** · NEW `test_backlog_leg1_diagnostics_s142.py` **28/28** · existing Sch 1-A suites + `test_diagnostics` **136/136** · all Topic-5 / 5329 suites **110/110** · state-refund + W-2G + 8889 + 8863 **76/76** · Topic 8 + 8995-A + Topic 5 + diagnostics core **152/152** · `test_flow_assertions` + `test_w2_unit2_diagnostics` **526/526** · `test_rule_metadata` + `test_seed_all_deploy_safety` + `test_1040_spine_diagnostics` green. `seed_rules` run on the DEMO DB (all 12 new rules active and importable, both promotions verified); **PROD seeds at Ken's deploy.** ⚠ **NEW LESSONS:** **a spec condition that cannot fire IS a finding** — read the compute path before trusting a condition, then write the rule against the inputs and flag the spec; **a year-keyed constant dict with a `.get(year, DEFAULT)` fallback is a silent wrong answer, not a safe default** — pin per year and SKIP the unpinned ones; **a nullable cap has THREE reachable states** (a value / an explicit 0 / NULL) meaning three different things, and both Form 8889 line 6 and the Form 5329 `*_value` caps are that shape; **a registry trip-wire that pins an exact code set fails on every new rule** — five were updated (`test_form_w2g_diagnostics_leg` and `test_form8889_diagnostics_leg` gained an `_ERROR` set; the Schedule C family went 21 → 22 across three files; 8863's severity), always with a pointer to the behaviour test and never by loosening the assertion; **a DIAGNOSTIC IS NOT A COMPUTE FIX** — four of these rules sit on top of defects still live in LEG 2, so each message says to refigure by hand; and the RS key for Schedule 1-A is **`SCH_1A`** (`1040_SCH1A`, `SCH1A`, `1040S1A`, `1040_SCH_1A` all 404 — a guessed-key 404 is NOT "no spec"). ▶ NEXT: **LEG 2**, starting with item 1, the stale QBI deduction on 1040 line 13 ($859) — smallest change, clearest proof, and `D_8995_STALE` already reports it. Each LEG 2 item needs its RS spec fetched first, `pytest tests/test_flow_assertions.py -v` after, and a Ken deploy.

## 🟢 **s141 (2026-07-30), unit 25: FORM 5329 / ADDITIONAL TAXES ON QUALIFIED PLANS (Parts I-IX) SHIPPED (`slate-ui`, no deploy).** **DOCUMENT TABS PER OWNER** — here the "one form per owner" ruling is literal: taxpayer and spouse each file a separate Form 5329, printed as its own copy and transmitted as its own e-file document. Inside a tab the nine parts are InputRow worksheets, the five excess-contribution parts share ONE generated block (same shape: a prior-year carryforward reduced by absorbing amounts, plus the current year's excess, taxed at 6% of the smaller of that total or the 12/31 account value), and the whole computed face renders from the server's own per-owner line dict as locked ƒx cells. ⚠ **THIS UNIT MADE ONE SERVER CHANGE, ADDITIVE AND READ-ONLY:** `Form5329Serializer.computed_lines` / `.computed_total` now expose the SAME `compute_5329_form_lines` dict the diagnostics, the renderer and the MeF extract already consume. It was necessary, not cosmetic — **no API published this form's arithmetic at all** (`compute_5329_db` persists only the TAXPAYER's Part I lines 1-4 to FormFieldValue, and Schedule 2 line 8 holds BOTH owners combined), which is exactly why the legacy screen re-implemented all seven part formulas itself in `previewIItoIX`; the sweep's standing ruling (units 17 and 19) is to RETIRE client re-implementations of the engine, not carry them forward. No migration; 7 new server tests; the 91 existing Topic-5 / 5329 / seed-leg tests still green. **SIX REAL DEFECTS:** ⚠⚠⚠ (1) **THE 25% SIMPLE RATE IS CHARGED ON THE WHOLE OF LINE 3, NOT THE SIMPLE PORTION.** The Part I caution on the 2025 form face reads "If any part of the amount on line 3 was a distribution from a SIMPLE IRA, you may have to include 25% of **that amount** on line 4 instead of 10%" — 25% belongs on the SIMPLE slice with 10% on the rest. `compute_5329_full` carries ONE boolean and multiplies the ENTIRE line 3 by it, and the boolean is auto-set from **any** code-S 1099-R on the return (`bool(row.simple_25pct) or has_s`), so the mixed case fires by itself with no preparer action. Engine-proven: 50,000 of code-1 early 401(k) money alongside 2,000 of code-S SIMPLE money gives line 4 = 25% × 52,000 = **13,000** against a correct blend of 10% × 50,000 + 25% × 2,000 = **5,500** — **$7,500 OVERSTATED** — and the distortion GROWS as the SIMPLE slice shrinks: 100,000 + 500 gives **25,125 vs 10,125**, $15,000 overstated. Live-proven on the demo QA return through the real API (line 1 52,000 / line 3 52,000 / line 4 13,000 → Schedule 2 line 8 **23,420**, balance due **47,024**). Direction OVERSTATEMENT — the client overpays. **The RS spec's R-5329-02 carries the same simplification**, so per the authoritative-source rule this is FLAGGED, never silently fixed → REVIEW_QUEUE (split line 1 by code, apportion the line-2 exception, blend the rates, then correct the spec). ⚠⚠ (2) **A BLANK 12/31 ACCOUNT VALUE TAXES THE FULL EXCESS AND AN EXPLICIT 0 TAXES NOTHING.** The six `*_value` fields are the form's only nullable inputs and `_excess_part` reads None as "no smaller-of cap". Live-proven on ONE cell, three states, all through the real API: a 7,000 traditional-IRA excess with the value at 3,000 → **180** of tax; an explicit **0** → **0**; cleared to null → **420**. So the most natural way to record "I don't have the year-end statement" costs the client money, typing 0 quietly asserts an emptied account, and nothing on screen distinguished the three. Six parts blank at once → 1,398. D_5329_003 is only a `warning` → REVIEW_QUEUE (raise to error). ⚠⚠ (3) **THE SCREEN RE-IMPLEMENTED THE ENGINE AND DISPLAYED A NUMBER THAT IS NOT THE 5329 TAX.** `previewIItoIX` duplicated all seven part formulas and deliberately EXCLUDED Part I, then labelled the result "Parts II-IX preview": engine-proven, Part I 2,000 + Parts II-IX 2,920 = an owner total of **4,920** while the screen showed **2,920**. There was also no line 1/3/4 display at all, no per-part result, no owner total and no Schedule 2 line 8. ⚠⚠ (4) **THE PART IX CORRECTION-WINDOW ROW IS WORTH 15 PERCENTAGE POINTS AND THE SCREEN NEVER SAID SO.** SECURE 2.0 §302 charges 10% when the shortfall is corrected inside the correction window and 25% otherwise; engine-proven, the same 40,000 missed RMD costs **4,000** on line 52a and **10,000** on line 52b — a 6,000 swing on which row it is keyed to. The legacy labels named the rows but never defined the window or the stake, and D_5329_004 is only `info`. The waiver route (attach a statement, pay nothing) cannot be expressed by the model at all. ⚠ (5) **THE SIMPLE CHECKBOX CAN READ FALSE WHILE THE ENGINE APPLIES 25%** — a code-S 1099-R forces the rate regardless of the box, so the screen showed an unticked box on a return taxed at 2.5× the displayed rate (live-confirmed). ⚠ (6) **D_RET_007, THE ONE DIAGNOSTIC THAT SAYS "VERIFY THE RATE APPLIED", IS NOT DUAL-AWARE** — it reads the DEPRECATED `Taxpayer.f5329_simple_25pct` scalar and the 1099-R codes, never the per-owner `Form5329.simple_25pct` row field, though `_f5329_state` in the same module is fully dual-aware and every other 5329 diagnostic uses it; ticking the box on the dual model with no code-S document gets the 25% silently, from the exact check written to catch it. Also surfaced at the cell that causes it: a stranded exception on the owner with no early distribution (the 10% is charged in full on the OTHER owner's form, D_5329_001), an exception amount with **no exception number** (the form requires it; nothing checked), an invalid exception number (D_RET_006), Part II line 6 above line 5 (D_5329_002), and a spouse form on a non-joint return. ⚠ **TWO DEFECTS IN THE NEW SCREEN ITSELF, caught by its own tests and its live run:** six excess parts shared ONE `aria-label` per cell ("12/31/2025 account value") — ambiguous to a screen reader and to `getByLabelText` alike — and the blank-cap ERROR PROSE was generic too, so six identical-looking errors could not be told apart; every per-part label and message now names its account. GATES: vitest **1114/1114** (+25) · tsc 46 = baseline · NEW `test_5329_computed_lines_s141.py` **7/7** · the 91 existing Topic-5 / 5329 / seed-leg tests still green · side-by-sides committed · all 3 QA PATCHes **200** (`tira_value` "3000" → "0" → `null`) · demo QA seed reverted to the exact s140 baseline with **ZERO drift**, ORM-verified line by line with print blockers empty. New tooling `scripts/qa_unit25.mjs`. ⚠ **NEW LESSONS:** a `SerializerMethodField` must NOT cache on the model instance — `refresh_from_db()` does not clear an ad-hoc attribute, and the unit's own test caught the stale read; **a screen repeating one label across N sibling blocks is an ACCESSIBILITY defect before it is a test problem** — fix the screen, not the query; and `FieldStateInput` correctly issues NOTHING when an already-blank cell is "cleared", so a nullable-commit test must start from a FILLED cell. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **Form 6251 (AMT)**, then 8615 / 1116 / 8880 / 8960 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab / the prior-year + summary views (~10 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane, Ken's call).

## 🟢 **s140 (2026-07-29), unit 24: SCHEDULE 1-A / THE FOUR OBBBA ADDITIONAL DEDUCTIONS SHIPPED (`slate-ui`, no deploy).** A **SINGLETON SCREENBAR** hosting three paradigms that NEST: InputRow worksheets for the Part II (tips) / Part III (overtime) return-level facts, **PayerTable** for the Part IV line-22 vehicle rows (the row's identity is the serializer-validated 17-character VIN; the two statutory attestations live in the expansion), a bare **`.slate-asstable`** over the two filers for Part V — which has NO inputs of its own, the birth dates living on Taxpayer Info and the valid-SSN flags on Credits & 8812, so the table annotates rather than edits — and the whole computed face from the server's OWN SCH_1A rows as locked ƒx cells, plus a threshold table showing all four parts' cap / phaseout start / rounding direction / deduction at once (four different thresholds, three different caps and TWO OPPOSITE rounding directions, none re-implemented on the client). **TWELVE REAL DEFECTS**, eleven from reading `compute_sch_1a.py`, the seeded RS spec, the pymupdf-extracted 2025 form face and the render + e-file legs against the JSX, and one from the live run: ⚠⚠⚠ (1) **A BLANK DATE OF BIRTH SILENTLY DISCARDS THE WHOLE SENIOR DEDUCTION AND NOTHING WARNS** — `_born_before(None, cutoff)` is False, so an unentered DOB is indistinguishable from "under 65"; engine-proven on identical returns (single, 70, AGI 80,000) line 35 computes **5,700 either way** while 36a / 37 / 38 and 1040 line 13b go **5,700 → 0**, and MFJ with two seniors at AGI 140,000 goes **12,000 → 6,000** on one blank spouse DOB — the per-person amount still PRINTS, so the filed page shows the deduction as available and $0 as claimed; **LIVE-PROVEN AT REST on the demo QA return with no seeding at all** (its taxpayer carries `date_of_birth = NULL`: MAGI 94,560, line 35 **4,826** available per person, line 37 **0**, line 38 **0**, the error firing by name on first paint; 1 of the 8 demo taxpayers is NULL). Direction UNDERSTATEMENT — the client overpays. ⚠⚠ (2) **THE TIPS DEDUCTION IGNORES `W2Income.owner`** — line 4a sums box 7 across EVERY W-2 as soon as *either* filer attests; the field exists (it drives the per-owner box-12 derive) and compute's comment claiming it doesn't is STALE; engine-proven MFJ AGI 120,000 with a spouse W-2 carrying 10,000 of box-7 tips: taxpayer-attests-only gives deduction **10,000**, identical to both attesting (mirror case identical). OVERSTATEMENT. ⚠⚠ (3) **SCHEDULE 1-A HAS NO DIAGNOSTICS AT ALL** — D_SCH1A_001..006 all unimplemented (two of them ERRORS: MFS claiming, and claiming with no valid SSN), no `rules_sch_1a.py` among the 80-odd rules modules, while Georgia's `rules_ga500.py` DOES police the HB 463 tips/overtime exclusions. ⚠⚠ (4) **THE SCHEDULE IS NEVER TRANSMITTED, BUT ITS TOTAL IS** — 1040 line 13b maps to `TotalAdditionalDeductionsAmt` and e-files, yet no `IRS1040Schedule1A` document is built (a grep of apps/efile for sch_1a / 1040s1a / QPVLI / overtime / senior returns NOTHING) while Schedules 1, 2, 3, A, B, D, E, 8812 and EIC all have builders; proof case: tips 4,000 + OT 5,000 + car 3,000 + age 75 e-files **17,700** with no supporting schedule. ⚠⚠ (5) **TIPS FROM MORE THAN ONE EMPLOYER BREAK THE FORM'S OWN INSTRUCTION** — the line 4 face text demands "enter -0- on lines 4a and 4b" and the engine prints the straight sum (two W-2s, 6,000 + 4,000 → 4a **10,000**); the spec's R-TIPS-01 is annotated "(single employer)" and D_SCH1A_006 exists for exactly this, unbuilt. ⚠ (6) **THE W-2 BOX 5 > $176,100 SPECIAL CASE IS IGNORED** though `medicare_wages` is already on the model. ⚠ (7) **BOTH PART IV ATTESTATIONS DEFAULT TO TRUE** — a row created with nothing but a VIN and an amount claims seven statutory conditions nobody affirmed (live-proven: the POST body was `{"vin":…,"total_interest_paid":"0"}` and the row came back with both boxes ticked); ORM-proven cost of one $1,200 vehicle: 13b 0 → 1,200, taxable income 78,810 → 77,610, tax 12,204 → 11,940, owed 10,151 → **9,876** — **$275 of tax**, where `tips_eligible_for_deduction` defaults False and the RS spec gives both car attestations a null default. ⚠ (8) **VEHICLES 3+ VANISH FROM THE PRINTED FORM WHILE LINE 23 SUMS THEM** (`render_sch_1a` takes `[:2]`) — three rows 1,200+900+700 print 2,100 against a line 23 of **2,800**, a filed page that contradicts itself; the >2-VIN statement page isn't built. ⚠ (9) **MFS ZEROES THREE OF THE FOUR PARTS AND PART IV SURVIVES** — identical facts, MFS **3,000** vs single **17,700**; verified against the form face, whose Part IV caution carries NO joint-return requirement while II, III and V all read "If married, you must file jointly." ⚠ (10) **THE SCREEN SHOWED NO COMPUTED RESULT AT ALL** and Parts I, V and VI were absent from it entirely. ⚠ (11) **THE CAR-LOAN PHASEOUT ROUNDS UP × $200** — the opposite direction and double the rate of tips/overtime; AGI 100,050 (fifty dollars over) costs a full **200**. ⚠ (12) **R-TIPS-10, THE §224 SE GROSS-INCOME LIMIT, IS UNBUILT** on a stale "awaits Schedule C" deferral. ⚠ Caught by the LIVE run and not the reading: the threshold table printed **"phaseout starts 0"** for tips, because the tips/overtime short-circuits return a dict of zeros that includes the threshold row itself — a DISENGAGEMENT zero rendered as a number is wrong guidance (the s136 lesson); the cell now shows an em dash and the constant is still never re-derived client-side. GATES: vitest **1089/1089** (+39) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed (`Design/slate-phase2-screenshots/{legacy,slate}-sch1a.png`) · every QA write **200/201/204** (POST 201 · 2 vehicle PATCH · 6 taxpayer PATCH · DELETE 204) · demo QA writes reverted to the exact s139 baseline with **ZERO drift**, ORM-verified line by line with print blockers empty. New tooling `scripts/qa_unit24.mjs` (needs no seed). ⚠ **NEW QA LESSONS:** the per-RECORD PATCH lane's re-render can lag a settled write by a WHOLE ACTION while the facts lane keeps up in the same run — **browser proves the screen, ORM + `compute_return` proves the numbers**; a PayerTable add row KEEPS ITS TEXT after a rejected add and headless typing APPENDS (clear with Ctrl+A then Backspace); **extract a form's own caution text with pymupdf before writing prose that quotes it** — three of unit 24's findings came from the PDF, not the spec. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **Form 5329**, then 6251 / 8615 / 1116 / 8880 / 8960 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab / the prior-year + summary views (~11 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane, Ken's call).

## 🟢 **s139 (2026-07-29), unit 23: THE EARNED INCOME CREDIT (§32) SHIPPED (`4c1e51c`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** A **SINGLETON SCREENBAR** hosting a bare `.slate-asstable` over the EXISTING Dependent rows for the qualifying-child determination (nothing is added or deleted here), InputRow worksheets for every §32 eligibility fact, and the whole computed face from the server's OWN 1040_EIC rows as locked ƒx cells; the tab's three stacked views share ONE `.slate-screen` at the call site (the s130 Schedule A ruling). **TWELVE REAL DEFECTS:** ⚠⚠⚠ (1) **"Self-employed at any time in the year" is the GATE that admits every dollar of SE income into the EIC, and UNANSWERED means NO** — `compute_earned_income` reaches Worksheet B only when the flag is truthy and `bool(None) is False`, so earned income is computed from WAGES ONLY and the Schedule C profit is discarded; engine-proven (HOH, no wages, Sch 1 L3 20,000 / L15 1,413) unanswered → earned **0**, CREDIT **0** vs Yes → earned **18,587**, CREDIT **4,328**, and with two qualifying children **7,152 vs 0** — **NO DIAGNOSTIC COVERS IT** (rules_eic carries no SE rule but the clergy defer, and nothing derives the flag from a Schedule C); live-proven on the demo return, answering Yes moved the server's own Worksheet B rows to **1e 18,587 / 4b 68,587** (earned income 50,000 → 68,587). ⚠⚠ (2) **the main-home exception label stated the OPPOSITE of Pub 596 Rule 14** — "NOT in the U.S. (incl. Puerto Rico / territories)" — and `eic_puerto_rico_territory_home`, the other arm of the SAME D_EIC_012 disqualifier, had **NO INPUT anywhere in the app** (typed on TaxpayerData, exposed by the serializer, absent from EIC_TAXPAYER_FIELDS); direction OVERSTATEMENT, and both are fixed in the shared const so the legacy screen is corrected by the same lines (live-proven `{"eic_puerto_rico_territory_home":true}` → 200). ⚠⚠ (3) **Form 2555 zeroes the EIC and its only checkbox lives on the Credits (8812) screen labelled for its ACTC effect only** — the unit-20 shape. ⚠⚠ (4) **Rule 2 (valid SSN) is fail-safe, invisible and un-overridable** — `is not True` disqualifies, and the fact is derived as a SERIALIZER SIDE EFFECT only when an SSN is present and only through the API, so an imported/seeded taxpayer keeps `null` forever; **DB-proven: 3 of the 7 demo taxpayers that HAVE an SSN carry eic_valid_ssn_taxpayer = NULL**. (5) an explicit 0 in the Worksheet B overrides is silently replaced by Schedule 1 (`_dec(fact) or _dec(sch1_l3)` — the unit-20 `or 12` shape). (6) answering ANY tri-state engages the whole EIC machinery and can raise eligibility ERRORs. (7) **the screen showed NO computed result at all** — no earned income, no investment total vs the §32(i) limit, no table lookup, no lower-of-AGI, no credit. (8) three seeded 1040_EIC rows (`step5_1`/`step5_2`/`wsb_6`) are NEVER written by the engine. (9) the investment-income note understated what counts — the engine adds the WHOLE 1040 line-7 capital gain (200 + 800 + a 12,000 stock gain = 13,000 > the 11,950 limit → no EIC). (10) the combat-pay election governs the EIC only — Schedule 8812 adds the same shared field to ACTC earned income unconditionally. (11) a flagged qualifying child's determinable §32(c)(3) failures and TIN type were invisible. ⚠⚠ (12) **A COMPUTE DEFECT FOUND BY THE REVERT, NOT THE SCREEN → REVIEW_QUEUE: deleting the last QBI source leaves a STALE QBI DEDUCTION on 1040 line 13.** `compute_8995_db`'s not-engaged branch blanks the 8995 rows but leaves line 13 untouched ("direct entry survives" — which `write_line_13`'s `is_overridden` guard already handles); reproduced cleanly on the demo return — add a Schedule C → line 13 **3,717**; delete it → AGI correctly back to 94,560 but line 13 **stays 3,717**, taxable income **75,093 instead of 78,810**, tax **11,379 instead of 12,204**, amount owed **9,292 instead of 10,151** — an **$859 understatement** with no qualified business income on the return; two further recomputes do not clear it and no diagnostic flags it (chip `task_8000a11e`). GATES: vitest **1050/1050** (+48) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed · every QA PATCH 200 (the render-pdf 400s are the §6695(g) print gate) · demo QA writes reverted to the exact s138 baseline with **ZERO drift**, ORM-verified line by line. ⚠ **THE REVERT IS A TEST, NOT HOUSEKEEPING** — compare EVERY line, not just the ones you touched: AGI came back exactly right while the deduction below it did not. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **Sch 1-A (OBBBA)**, then 5329 / 6251 / 8615 / 1116 / 8880 / 8960 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab (~12 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane, Ken's call).

## 🟢 **s138 (2026-07-29), unit 22: FORM 8863 / EDUCATION CREDITS (§25A) SHIPPED (`54a3d04`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** **DOCUMENT TABS** per student — Part III is one full page per student (the unit-19 per-owner ruling) — with an InputRow worksheet for the expenses and the four eligibility answers, a nested institution block, and the computed face from the server's OWN FORM_8863 rows as locked ƒx cells. **SEVEN REAL DEFECTS:** ⚠⚠ (1) **one student with BOTH expense boxes gets BOTH credits** — `sum_l30` runs over AOTC-eligible students and `sum_llc` over ALL students independently, so the same money earns both; §25A(c)(2)(A) forbids it, engine-proven L19 **2,300 vs 1,500** (an extra $800), and LIVE-proven through the real API with line 1 at 2,500 while line 10 became 4,000 — the same $4,000 counted twice at once. Legacy's own prose claimed the rule was enforced and D_8863_DUAL_STUDENT is only a warning. ⚠⚠ (2) **the "→ LLC only" labels promised a routing that never happens** — `_aotc_eligible` merely EXCLUDES the student from the AOTC sum and nothing moves the money; live-proven, the tick collapsed line 1 from 2,500 to **0** with line 10 still 0, the expenses simply vanished (L19 0 vs 800 if moved). ⚠⚠ (3) **the line-7 lockout is a per-student checkbox with a RETURN-WIDE effect** (`any(...)`) — two students, ticked on one, and the refundable **$2,000 for BOTH** goes nonrefundable. (4) **clearing either expense box was a silent revert** — serializer-proven `""` → 400 and `null` → 400, only `"0"` works. (5) **a student with no 9-digit SSN makes the return UNFILEABLE** (the extract raises). (6) **MFS / a dependent filer bars both credits** and the screen just showed "$0.00". (7) **there was no computed face** beyond lines 8 and 19 — the phaseout was invisible. GATES: vitest **1002/1002** (+82 across the three s138 units) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed · all 6 QA PATCHes 200 · demo QA writes reverted to the exact baseline. ⚠ One engine asymmetry found by the revert: **a `disengage()` guarded on `!= ZERO` cannot clear a "0" it wrote itself** (Sch 3 line 3 kept a printed 0) → REVIEW_QUEUE. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **EIC**, then Sch 1-A / 8880 / 8615 / 5329 / 6251 / 8960 / 1116 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab (~13 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane, Ken's call).

## 🟢 **s138 (2026-07-29), unit 21: FORM 8962 / PREMIUM TAX CREDIT (§36B) SHIPPED (`29b2fc4`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** **DOCUMENT TABS** per Form 1095-A (a received information return with its own policy identity and 36 monthly boxes — the W-2 archetype), the 12 × 3 monthly grid and the Part IV allocation rows as nested `.slate-asstable` blocks, an InputRow facts worksheet, and the computed face from the server's OWN FORM_8962 rows as locked ƒx cells. **EIGHT REAL DEFECTS, one of them the worst the sweep has found:** ⚠⚠⚠ (1) **a blank line 11(f) reports the WHOLE advance PTC as ZERO.** The annual method (line 10 Yes) reads only the line 11 year totals and ignores the 1095-A entirely; `D_8962_ANNUAL_INCOMPLETE` checks only 11(a)/11(b) and **explicitly permits** a blank 11(f), while `D_8962_ANNUAL_CONFLICT` only says the monthly amounts are "ignored" — so **nothing flags it**. Live-proven on the demo return, three states of the SAME 1095-A (12 × 1,000 / 1,100 / 900): monthly truth **REPAY 5,940, due 16,340**; 11(f) filled **REPAY 5,939, due 16,339**; **11(f) BLANK → APTC 0, a REFUNDABLE 4,861 on Sch 3 line 9, due 5,290** — the federal balance swings **11,050** and a repayment becomes a refund. ⚠⚠ (2) **clearing any of the 36 monthly cells was a silent revert** — serializer-proven `""` → 400 "A valid number is required" and `null` → 400 "may not be null"; only "0" is accepted, and CurrencyInput renders 0 as BLANK so clearing feels natural. ⚠⚠ (3) **the SEHI checkbox said "flagged" for something fully computed** — it runs the Pub 974 iterative and REWRITES Sch 1 line 17. (4) a month with no column A earns no credit but still repays its APTC (975 owed, no credit). (5) **no 100%-of-FPL eligibility floor** — full credit at 66% of FPL where §36B is 100–400% → REVIEW_QUEUE (nuanced: the §1.36B-2(b)(6) safe harbour makes it right for the common APTC case). (6) the 400% cliff invisible (60,000 → cap 1,625; 60,241 → no cap). (7) a 2026 return silently computes nothing. (8) no computed face at all. GATES: vitest **974/974** (+54 across units 20+21) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed · every QA PATCH 200 · demo QA writes reverted with **ZERO drift**, twice. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **the education credits (Form 8863)**, then EIC / Sch 1-A / 8880 / 8615 / 5329 / 6251 / 8960 / 1116 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab (~14 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane).

## 🟢 **s138 (2026-07-29), unit 20: FORM 2441 / CHILD AND DEPENDENT CARE (§21) SHIPPED (`c7fa893`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** A **SINGLETON SCREENBAR** hosting three paradigms that NEST: a bare `.slate-asstable` over the EXISTING Dependent rows for Part II (nothing is added or deleted here, so a PayerTable add row would be a lie), **PayerTable** for the Part I providers with the address block in the expansion, an **InputRow** facts worksheet, and the whole computed face from the server's OWN FORM_2441 rows as locked ƒx cells. **SEVEN REAL DEFECTS:** ⚠⚠ (1) **the credit's gate lived on ANOTHER TAB and this screen said "✓ qualifies" anyway** — `qualifying_dependents()` requires `Dependent.claims_dependent_care`, but the legacy `qualifies()` tested only under-13/disabled and that claim-BLIND count drove the displayed "N qualifying persons → expense cap $X"; engine-proven on identical facts, **line 3 3,000 / CREDIT 600 claimed vs 0 / 0 unclaimed**, and live-proven with the form FULLY DISENGAGED (0 FORM_2441 rows, Sch 3 line 2 blank) while legacy printed "✓ qualifies · expense cap $3,000" (see the committed `legacy-f2441.png`); D_2441_007 exists but is only `info`. The flag is now editable AT THE ROW. ⚠⚠ (2) **the engine deems BOTH spouses for the same months** — i2441 lines 4/5 allow only ONE per month; engine-proven **CREDIT 1,920 vs 0**; the RS spec and source brief are both SILENT, so FLAGGED not filled → REVIEW_QUEUE. (3) **no ZIP input existed** though `zip_code` is printed in column (b) AND transmitted as the e-file `ZIPCd`. (4) **the tax-exempt checkbox produced an UNFILEABLE return** — `render_2441` prints column (c) blank and the e-file extract RAISES without a 9-digit TIN; i2441 wants the literal "Tax-Exempt" → the screen now says so, e-file mapping → REVIEW_QUEUE. (5) **only THREE Part I / Part II rows print** (`[:3]`), silently. (6) **"Months (max 12)" disagreed with the engine BOTH ways** — `min(12, int(stored or 12))` reads a stored **0 as 12** (deeming $3,000, not $0) and clamps 20. (7) **the screen showed NO computed result at all.** GATES: vitest **947/947** (+27) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed · all 7 QA PATCHes 200 · demo QA writes reverted with **ZERO drift**. ▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at **Form 8962 (PTC)**, then 8863 / EIC / Sch 1-A / 8880 / 8615 / 5329 / 6251 / 8960 / 1116 / 5695 / 1040-X / the state tab (~15 of ~39 1040 screens remain; the business-entity screens are a separate unscoped lane, Ken's call).

## 🟢 **s137 (2026-07-29), units 18 + 19: MISCELLANEOUS INCOME / FORM W-2G (`9c379e7`) and FORM 8889 / HSA (`5f2ac27`) SHIPPED** (`slate-ui`, no deploy). Both take **DOCUMENT TABS**: a W-2G carries its own payer identity and eight boxes and e-files as its own IRSW2G document; "one Form 8889 per owner" is the unit-12 ruling. Both now render their computed face from the server's OWN rows (FORM_W2G, FORM_8889) as locked ƒx cells — unit 18 retired a client-side sum that its own footnote admitted was not authoritative, unit 19 retired a client copy of the §223 limit table AND a re-implementation of the deduction chain. **FOUR MORE REAL DEFECTS:** ⚠⚠ (1) **the gambling loss deduction silently vanished** — Sch A line 16 is `min(pct × scha_gambling_losses, scha_gambling_winnings)`, capped by a SEPARATE fact nothing populates from the W-2G docs and which lived only on the Deductions tab, so the screen's own instruction ("enter losses once here — it flows to Schedule A automatically") produced a **ZERO deduction** while the winnings sat in income; engine-proven `gambling_line16(0,10000,0,2025)=0` vs `(10000,…)=10000`, and the screen's red warning compared against the REAL winnings so it actively reassured the preparer. ⚠⚠ (2) **the HSA deduction collapsed when an "optional" field was cleared** — `family_allocation` is the ONE nullable field on HSAAccount and the engine branches on `is not None`, so blank means "take the whole prorated limit", but legacy committed `value || "0"` for every box; live-proven through the server's own line 13: **8,550 → 0** on `{"family_allocation":"0"}`, back to 8,550 on `null` — and the $0 share ALSO tripped D_8889_EXCESS ("exceed the deductible limit of 0"), a cascade instead of a cause. (3) **"box 15 is informational" was FALSE** — it feeds the Sch A line-5a state-withholding deduction (live: 500 → Sch A 5a 1,400). (4) **the §165(d) note ignored the OBBBA haircut** (`GAMBLING_LOSS_PCT = {2025:1.00, 2026:0.90}`) — now year-aware. Also surfaced at the cause: FW2G-009-01 (nonzero box 14 needs BOTH box-13 halves, only the missing half flagged), D_W2G_WH_ONLY, D_8889_EXCESS/FUNDING/MEDICARE/HDHP/TY2026, an eligible-months entry above 12 (the engine clamps it silently). ⚠ **`compute_8889_db` stores the FORM_8889 face from accounts[0] ONLY** — the PDF is right per owner (`render_8889` recomputes), so the screen shows the face for the first account and points a later owner at the printed form; REVIEW_QUEUE. ⚠ Fixed an **addressability** defect the tests caught: the payer's State and box 13's State had IDENTICAL aria labels. ⚠⚠ **THE REMAINING-SCREEN LIST WAS ABOUT HALF THE REAL COUNT** — measured by mapping every `activeTab` to its section and checking each for a NEW_UI gate (resolving components to their own FILES; scanning FormEditor alone wrongly reports extracted sections as unconverted): **~23 of ~39 1040 screens converted, ~16 left**, not the 8 past lists named; the omitted ones are 8880/8615/5329/6251/8960/1116/Sch 1-A/1040-X/state/summary views. The BUSINESS-ENTITY screens (~12) are a separate unscoped lane. Gates: **vitest 920/920** (+35 across the three s137 units) · tsc 46 = baseline (verified by diffing a clean worktree at HEAD, not a remembered count) · side-by-sides committed. ▶ **NEXT: continue the sweep at Form 2441 (child care)**, then 8962, 8863, EIC, Sch 1-A, 8880, 8615, 5329, 6251, 8960, 1116, 5695, 1040-X, the state tab, estimates/extension/e-file.

## 🟢 **s137 (2026-07-29), unit 17: THE STATE REFUND WORKSHEET (§111) SHIPPED (`f984261`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** Every input is an `sr_*` fact on the Taxpayer — no record collection — so it takes the **s132 SINGLETON**: screenbar + two-column InputRow worksheet, the ONE taxpayer-facts PATCH left in `StateRefundSection` (view over container). **The whole Pub 525 Worksheet 2/2a chain now renders from the server's OWN `STATE_REFUND` rows as locked ƒx cells** — the client never re-implements the prior-year standard-deduction table; the legacy screen discarded those twelve computed lines and printed two numbers. **THREE REAL DEFECTS, all found by reading the engine against the screen:** (1) `sr_py_age_blind_boxes` was missing from `SR_DECIMAL_FIELDS`, so clearing it sent `""` — and it is an **IntegerField**, which DRF rejects ("A valid integer is required") → a 400 and a silent revert; proven at the serializer and live (`{"sr_py_age_blind_boxes":"0"}` → 200), and the legacy rendering is fixed by the same line. (2) **A Pub 525 exception read "$0 taxable"** — with any exception flag on, `compute_state_refund_db` DISENGAGES and blanks Sch 1 line 1/8z, yet the legacy panel still printed "Taxable refund: $0.00"; the preparer was told zero when the answer is "refigure by hand". (3) ⚠⚠ **A blank prior-year Sch A line 5e silently zeroes the ENTIRE refund** — `worksheet_2a` takes the cap-limited branch whenever 5d > 5e, so with 5e at 0 the recapture is all of 5d, `a3 <= a4` always holds, and it returns (0, 0). **No diagnostic covers it** (D_SR_INCOMPLETE checks only line 17 and 5d). Proven on the engine: same inputs, 5e blank turns **$1,200 of taxable income into $0**. Now an invalid overlay + alert on the 5e cell, while the GENUINE cap-limited zero gets its own explanatory note so a real $0 is never mysterious. Also surfaced at the cause: D_SR_INCOMPLETE on 5d/17, the §111 standard-deduction / sales-tax gate, the server's own w8 outcome, the 8z routing note, and an over-4 age/blind count (`py_standard_deduction` multiplies it through with **no ceiling** and the serializer does not cap it). The prior year is named everywhere (2024 on a TY2025 return). Live QA reverted + ORM-verified: 2024 itemized $40,000, SALT uncapped $9,000, $1,200 refund → a3 1,200 / a5 1,200 / 1a 1,200 / w7 29,200 / w8 10,800 / w11 1,200 → Sch 1 L1 1,200 → **AGI 94,560 → 95,760**; reverted through the screen's own lane to AGI 94,560 / L15 78,810 / L16 12,204 / L25b 2,450. Gates: **vitest 897/897** (+12) · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-sides committed. ▶ **NEXT: continue the sweep at Misc Income**, then HSA 8889, EIC, 2441, 8962, education, 5695, estimates/extension/e-file cards.

## 🟢 **s136 (2026-07-29), unit 16: FORM 1099-G (UNEMPLOYMENT §85) SHIPPED (`e030b71`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** Many state agencies, one number each = the INT/DIV **PayerTable slim grid**; the legacy draft card `__draft_1099g__` is RETIRED under the flag by the s130 type-to-add ruling (the typed payer name IS the create payload). ⚠⚠ **TRAP AVOIDED — check a model's `OWNER_CHOICES` before wiring PayerTable's `showOwner`:** `Form1099G` allows **taxpayer/spouse only**, while the shared owner cell offers a third **"Joint"** — correct for InterestIncome / DividendIncome / CapitalTransaction (`IncomeOwner` / `CapitalOwner` carry it) and a **400-and-silent-revert** here; this screen renders its own owner cell. **No ƒx cells**; blank map = five money columns → `"0"`, `payer_name` is `blank=True` → `""` with the blank guard OFF (proven live). The row preview nets exactly as `compute_1099g.net_unemployment` (box 1 less the SAME-YEAR repayment, **floored at zero**), and three engine conditions the legacy screen never surfaced at the row now show where they are caused — **D_1099G_WH_ONLY** as an invalid overlay on the box-4 cell, **D_1099G_1341** and **D_1099G_OTHERBOXES** as alerts in the expansion. The legacy header text was re-checked against the engine and **is accurate** (the unit-15 audit re-run, passing). **Live QA, reverted, ORM-verified:** "GA Dept of Labor" box 1 $6,000 / $600 withheld → Sch 1 line 7 6,000 → **AGI 94,560 → 100,560**, L16 12,204 → 13,524, **L25b 2,450 → 3,050**; a $1,500 same-year repayment nets the row to 4,500; box 1 cleared to 0 raises the box-4 overlay + alert and both clear when it returns; deleted through its own lane (204) → back at rest. Gates: vitest **885/885** · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-side committed. NEW `scripts/qa_unit16_{entry,revert}.mjs`. **▶ NEXT: the sweep continues at State Refund**, then Misc Income · HSA 8889 · EIC · 2441 · 8962 · education · 5695 · the estimates/extension/e-file cards.

## 🟢 **s136 (2026-07-29), unit 15: THE SS LUMP-SUM ELECTION SHIPPED (Pub 915 Worksheets 2 + 4, `53dfd5b`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** An earlier-year row is NOT a separately filed form — one preparer-asserted fact set per year, keyed by row id and a few numbers wide — so it takes the **PayerTable slim grid + expansion**, the unit-12 ruling's counterpart, with **type-to-add** (the typed year IS the create payload) replacing the legacy add button + separate edit panel. **NO ƒx cells** (compute_ss_lumpsum re-derives every line; the WS1-vs-WS4 comparison reaches the preparer via D_RET_008). Blank map: `earlier_year` is `null=True` → blank commits **null** (so PayerTable's payer-name blank guard is OFF), the six money columns commit **"0"** — both proven live. **THREE stale-or-wrong GUIDANCE defects fixed, all found by reading the engine against the screen: (1)** the RED note fired on *(election && no rows)* but **D_RET_004 gates on the SSA-1099 INDICATOR and on "no row carries a positive amount"** — a preparer with the election on, a row added and $0 in it saw the warning vanish while the diagnostic still fired RED; **(2)** the note claimed WS4 supersedes 6b *"only if it is lower"* — it supersedes **whenever the toggle is on**, and D_RET_008 warns rather than reverting; **(3)** the Social Security screen (Slate AND legacy) still said the election **"is not yet supported — prepare manually"** when it has been COMPUTED since the retiree-hardening cluster. Both now point at this screen and keep the one true caveat: pre-1994 years take Worksheet 3, which isn't built — and since `earlier_year` is a LABEL the engine never reads, a 1993 row would run silently through WS2, so it now carries the invalid overlay + an alert. **Live QA, reverted, ORM-verified** (the classic disability back-payment): indicator on, 2023 carrying $9,000 of the $21,600 box 5, that year's box 5 $12,000 vs AGI $8,000 → the earlier year stays under the §86 base so WS2 adds nothing and this year re-runs on $12,600 → **1040 L6b 18,360 → 10,710**, AGI **94,560 → 86,910**, L16 12,204 → 10,521 (savings 7,650, beneficial, applied) → deleted through its own lane (204) → back at rest. Gates: vitest **876/876** · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-side committed. NEW `scripts/qa_unit15_{entry,revert}.mjs`. ⚠⚠ **A checkbox click TOGGLES** — an unconditional click flips the flag back OFF on a second run (that is exactly what "the indicator never saved" looked like for two runs); both toggles are idempotent now and assert their state. ⚠ The taxpayer-facts lane is `/taxpayer/`, NOT `/info/`. ⚠ The SS screen's checkboxes have **no aria-label** — drive them by the wrapping label's text. ⚠ A QA `.mjs` must sit **under the repo root** (puppeteer-core is an ephemeral root install).

## 🟢 **s136 (2026-07-29), unit 14: FORM 8915-F SHIPPED — FORM 8915-F as document tabs (`3d57e5d`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** "One form per disaster year (item B) per spouse — never combine spouses", so the unit-12 DOCUMENT ruling applies: one tab per owner/year over the archetype two-column worksheet. **Item C (the FEMA disasters) is the exception inside the exception** — a nested list with REPLACE-ALL writes whose rows may not have an id yet, so it renders as a bare dense `.slate-asstable`, NOT a PayerTable (which is keyed by row id). **TWO REAL DEFECTS FIXED, both surfaced by live QA: (1) blank-commit** — legacy sent `null` for every blanked money field, but only the four derived-override columns are `null=True`; on the other sixteen the serializer answers "This field may not be null" (a 400), so clearing a value silently reverted. Non-nullable now commits `"0"`; proven live, the clear body is `{"cost_p2":"0"}` → 200. **(2) item-C clobber (silent data loss)** — every item-C write ships the COMPLETE list and that list came from the render closure; a PATCH here costs SECONDS, so keying the FEMA number then tabbing straight into the declaration date sent the pre-FEMA list and WIPED the number just saved. The list now composes through a ref (props stay authoritative). Reproduced live before the fix, clean after. Every `analysis` figure is a locked ƒx cell (same `compute_8915f` as the print leg); problems/e-file blockers/the opt-out mismatch are `role="alert"` errors raising the tab's error dot. **Live QA, fully reverted, ORM-verified:** DR-4832-GA, $22,000 of the $24,000 TRS pension designated a QDD → spread ÷3 = 7,333 + the 2,000 non-QDD remainder → 1040 L5b **24,000 → 9,333**, AGI **94,560 → 79,893**; deleted through the screen's own Remove lane (204) → back to AGI 94,560 / L15 78,810 / L16 12,204. Gates: vitest **867/867** · tsc 46 = baseline · side-by-side committed. NEW `scripts/qa_unit14_{entry,revert}.mjs`. ⚠⚠ Driver lessons now in-file: **`Ctrl+A` only SELECTS** (clearing needs an explicit `Backspace`, or a driver awaiting that write HANGS FOREVER), and **settling on "no write ISSUED for N ms" is a no-op** when the PATCH itself takes tens of seconds — count IN-FLIGHT writes instead.

## 🟢 **s135 (2026-07-29): SWEEP UNIT 13 SHIPPED — FORM 8606 + the Roth basis tracker (`9e88fdd`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** The 8606 tab carries TWO views, so it follows the Schedule A rule: ONE `.slate-screen` at the call site, neither view owning a root. Form 8606 takes the unit-12 document ruling (taxpayer and spouse each file their own), one tab per owner; the Roth basis tracker is a standing per-owner account of at most two rows × three fields — a facts worksheet with add buttons gated on which owners already have one. TWO REAL DEFECTS FIXED: an ENTRY GAP (line 4 and its companion had no input), and **the taxable PREVIEW ignored the carry it advertises** — lines 22/24 resolve from the per-owner tracker unless the 8606 field is non-zero, and the client preview read the raw fields (showed 11,510 vs the server's 6,510). Gates at s135: vitest 857/857 · tsc 46. *(s135 shipped without a session close; s136 recorded it.)*

## 🟢 **s134 (2026-07-29): SWEEP UNIT 12 SHIPPED — FORMS 6252 / 8824 / 7217, three screens on ONE paradigm ruling (`8ff8de4`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** A row that is itself a separately FILED form (i6252 "one Form 6252 per sale", i8824 "one per exchange", i7217 "a separate Form 7217 for each date") is a **DOCUMENT** → document tabs over the archetype two-column worksheet, never PayerTable (which is shaped for one-number-wide payer rows); the legacy summary tables fold into the tab labels + tools-bar totals. **Form 7217 NESTS the two paradigms for the first time**: the tabs carry the form (Part I facts as the worksheet) and its Part II distributed-property grid IS a flat record list → a nested PayerTable with type-to-add (the typed column (a) description is the create payload; `addProperty` gained an optional `description`). **View over container** — create / per-field PATCH / delete / the two-level 7217 property CRUD all stay in the legacy sections; new shared `useActiveRecord` keeps the settled rows[0]-when-stale rule and adds the legacy "Add → the edit form opens" contract (a record id absent last render becomes the active tab, because rows APPEND and rows[0] would strand a new one). **Blank-commit map verified field by field against `models.py`**: non-nullable money → `"0"` · **6252 `prior_gross_profit_pct`/`section_6621_rate` → `null`** (fractions, not currency — a 0% ratio is not "unanswered") · **8824 `days_to_identify`/`days_to_receive` → `null`** (null = not a deferred exchange; 0 days is a different real answer) · **7217 `outside_basis` → `null`** (unanswered = D_7217_004 RED; an entered 0 is valid) · `holding_period_months` → `0` · description/`rp_exception`/`category` → `""`. **NO ƒx cells on any of the three** — no serializer exposes a computed column (compute_6252/_8824/_7217 re-derive every line at render); the 7217 tri-states keep unanswered as a real third state with the invalid overlay, and a red-defer flag (6252 ×2, 8824 ×3) or an unanswered required assertion raises the tab's ERROR dot. **Two primitive fixes carried by the unit**: a new `.slate-select.is-invalid` overlay, and `.slate-inputrow-value > .slate-select` now pins to the 160px value column — a native select sizes to its longest option and a flex item won't shrink below content, so long option lists were bleeding over the next worksheet column on EVERY screen. **Live-proven on the demo QA return and fully reverted, every figure ORM-verified**: 6252 QA Duplex 300,000 / basis 180,000 / payments 60,000 → gross profit 120,000 ÷ contract price 300,000 = ratio 0.40 → **installment income 24,000** → Sch D → 1040 L7 150→24,150, total income 94,560→**118,560**; 8824 QA Warehouse cash 25,000 / FMV lk 500,000 / basis lk 320,000 → realized 205,000, **recognized = boot 25,000** → 4797 L5 §1231 → Sch D → L7 **49,150**, total income → **143,560**; 7217 QA Elm Street Partners + one property row, cash 5,000 < outside basis 40,000 → **no §731(a)(1) gain, no income change** (correct). Each deleted through the screen's own Delete lane (204) → back to AGI 94,560 / L15 78,810 / L16 12,204. Gates: vitest **844/844** (+31 new) · tsc **46 = baseline** · 3 side-by-sides committed. ⚠ New QA scripts `scripts/qa_unit12_{entry,revert}.mjs <returnId> <tokenFile> <6252|8824|7217>`; `slate_screen_screenshots.mjs` now accepts a **table-only** legacy section as ready (the 6252/8824 legacy screens render NO inputs until "Edit"). ⚠ **A rail label passed to that driver is a REGEX** — `"Schedule F (Farm)"` silently never matches. ⚠ **The legacy 6252/8824 delete lanes go through `window.confirm`** and puppeteer AUTO-DISMISSES an unhandled dialog — a revert driver without `page.on("dialog", d => d.accept())` is a silent no-op. ⚠ **Judge a delete by the tab count SETTLING, not a flat sleep** (a recompute-heavy delete outran a 3s pause and looked like a stale-tab defect). ⚠ **PowerShell mangles a multi-line `manage.py shell -c` string** — run ORM probes from bash with `shell -c "$(cat file)"`. ▶ NEXT: **the sweep continues at 8606**, then 8915-F / SS lump-sum / 1099-G / State Refund / Misc Income / HSA 8889 / EIC / 2441 / 8962 / education / 5695 / estimates-extension-e-file cards.

## (s133 below) — **SWEEP UNIT 11 SHIPPED — DEPRECIATION, the largest bespoke screen (`51e489b`, `slate-ui`, no deploy).** SlateDepreciationScreen converges ~1,900 lines (section + edit form + Form 4562 Part I card) onto a **THIRD paradigm: the ASSET REGISTER**. A conversion return carries 40+ assets whose columns are almost entirely server-computed and whose rows are a sub-schedule **per destination activity**, sub-grouped by asset class with its own subtotal and a "→ where it lands" band — neither PayerTable (flat one-line payers) nor DocumentTabs (a stack of per-document cards) can express that, so the register is its own renderer: screenbar action cluster · §168(k)(7) election span · paste/import panels · filter + bulk toolbar · per-activity `.slate-asstable` blocks · the archetype two-column InputRow worksheet for the selected asset · summary · Part I. **View over container, verified non-drifting:** every lane (s107 draft row, per-asset FIFO save queue, create-once serialisation, import preview/commit, CSV template/export, bulk assignment, the election PATCH, delete) stays in `DepreciationSection`, and the derived values (displayAssets/filteredAssets/activityGroups/totals) are computed ONCE and consumed by BOTH renderings. **Blank-commit map verified field by field against `models.py`**: non-nullable money → `"0"` · nullable money/lives/dates → `null` · description → `""` · **`business_pct`/`bonus_pct` → REFUSED** (non-nullable with meaningful defaults 100/0; legacy sent `""`, which 400s and reverts, so refusing client-side is legacy’s EFFECT without the failed request — typing 0 still sets zero). Every serializer `read_only_fields` value is a **locked ƒx cell** (`noOverride`); the AMT collapsible is FLATTENED (density-first); vehicle/amortization keep their GROUP conditions; disposal keeps its legacy gate. **Form 4562 Part I §179 got a Slate span inside the lazy chunk** and its taxpayer PATCH was lifted to the container, so ONE lane serves both renderings and no retired GREEN/YELLOW markup survives on a converged screen; the group/method/convention/life lists + group-defaults table became shared consts (two duplicated inline group lists collapsed). **Live-proven on the demo QA return and fully reverted:** Schedule C fixture → + Add asset → draft "unsaved" → description created the row in **exactly ONE POST** (201) → basis 50,000 / PIS 2025-03-03 / 200DB-HY-7 → **current depreciation 7,145** (Pub 946 Table A-1 year-one 14.29%) as a locked ƒx cell → activity band "$7,145 → Schedule C, Line 13" → **Sch C line 13 → SCH_1 L3 → 1040 L8 → AGI 94,560 − 7,145 = 87,415, all ORM-verified** → deleted through the screen’s own Del lane (204) → empty register → fixture removed → AGI back to 94,560. Gates: vitest **813/813** (+31 new) · tsc **46 = baseline** · 2 side-by-sides committed. ⚠ **On a 1040 Depreciation is NOT a rail item** — it is an activity sub-screen reached from "Open depreciation worksheet →" on Sch C/E/F (the tab id lives only in `SECTION_TABS`/`PARTNERSHIP_TABS`); new recipe `scripts/slate_depr_screenshots.mjs` + `scripts/qa_depr_{entry,revert}.mjs`. ⚠ **CORRECTION to s132’s close figure:** the QA return’s at-rest 1040 line 16 is **12,204**, not the 10,785 s132 recorded — that was a STALE stored value (its final ORM delete of the inert Schedule J row ran without a recompute). Confirmed stable, idempotent, hand-checked, and ruled out as a Schedule J defect (an inert row leaves line 16 at 12,204 both ways). **Lesson: an ORM delete on the QA return must be followed by `compute_return(tr)` before any figure is recorded as a baseline.** ▶ NEXT: **the sweep continues at 6252 / 8824 / 7217**, then 8606 / 8915-F / SS lump-sum / 1099-G / State Refund / Misc Income / HSA 8889 / EIC / 2441 / 8962 / education / 5695 / estimates-extension-e-file cards.

on the single-retiree return, entering the packet's prior-year figures moves the penalty **189 → 0** with nothing on screen saying the safe harbor did it. **THREE defects the audit found, none previously logged:** **(1) the stored Part III line numbers came from a SUPERSEDED Form 2210** — the 2025 face runs Section A as lines 10-18 (10 = required installment, 17 = underpayment, 18 = OVERPAYMENT) while the app stored the installment on "18" and the underpayment on "25", a line Part III does not have (25 is Schedule AI); RS carried the identical numbering; confirmed three independent ways — the face text, the widget grid (exactly nine 4-column rows + line 19), and the IRS template's OWN subform names `SectionATable[0].Line10[0]`…`Line18[0]`; a $5,000 payment in period 3 against $2,500 installments used to report periods 3-4 as paid when the form reports all four short. **No penalty changed** — Section B already applied payments earliest-first. **KEN CORRECTED MY PREMISE and was right:** I had said the 2210 is never transmitted, citing s75. IRS rule **F2210-002-02** (Active/Reject, read from the business-rules CSV this repo holds) says a transmitted 2210 must carry one of Part II boxes A-E, and the face says "You must file Form 2210" whenever one applies — the form IS filed in real cases. What is true is only that THIS APP transmits none (models box C alone, then refuses at extract). That s75 note is still unratified in REVIEW_QUEUE; I had cited it back to Ken as his decision, which it was not. Box D — withholding credited on ACTUAL dates — is an unmodeled penalty-REDUCING election, now queued. **Line 17 is a RUNNING outstanding balance, not each period's own shortfall** — my first authored expectation was wrong and the harness caught it; both readings integrate to identical amount-days, and the harness now pins that Section A ↔ Section B tie by two independent routes. **ALSO SHIPPED:** the documented source override — a controlling outside figure recorded WITHOUT discarding the computed penalty, moving 2210 line 19 and 1040 line 38 TOGETHER per F2210-006-01 (overriding line 38 alone splits them, which is what the QA preparer had to do); `D_2210_TIE` catches that workaround. The four real gaps, all closed: **(1) RECONCILIATION (RS `R-8283-RECON` / `D_8283_017`)** — `R-8283-SCHA12` lets a flat Schedule A line-12 entry override the 8283 row total per field (deliberate, unchanged), but the override was **SILENT**: the printed Form 8283 carried the rows, line 12 carried the override, and NOTHING compared them, so a TaxWise conversion that kept the packet total and keyed only part of the detail claimed the difference with no form item behind it (the 1065 already had this guard as `D_8283_016`; the 1040 did not). **Effect-scaled** per the `D_4562_DEST` convention: error when line 12 exceeds the non-withheld row total with NO withheld row present · warning on the reversed delta · **warning whenever a conservation/historic row is present** (`D_8283_006` withholds those and has the preparer key the allowable amount by hand, so a gap is EXPECTED — erroring would red-flag every correctly-prepared easement return). ## ▶ PREVIOUS — **s110 (2026-07-25, autonomous "go" — the top unblocked spine item): FORM 5695 COMPLETED TO THE LIGHT 2025 FACE.** Ken's scope call, verbatim: *"I think this form goes away for 2026 so you can do a light version. something good enough to complete the tax return."* Migration `returns.0211` (25 additive columns, both DBs); RS `9f2c8c0`. **THE AUDIT CAME FIRST and found a THIRD variant of the standing lesson:** all four 5695 legs were green with 38 passing tests — and the form still could not be filed, because the tests only ever exercised what the June "minimal v1" chose to model. Compared line by line against our own 2025 IRS template, v1 modelled **17 cost boxes and nothing else**. **(1) THE ELIGIBILITY GATES** (5a · 7a · 17a-c · 17e · 21a-b · 25a · 26a) are modelled **TRI-STATE**: an explicit No denies exactly the branch the face says to skip; an **UNANSWERED gate deliberately does NOT deny** — the app back-enters returns prepared elsewhere, where eligibility was settled off-system, so silently deleting a credit the preparer computed is worse than flagging a blank (`D_5695_GATE_OPEN`). ⚠⚠ **A No on 21a/21b skips lines 22-25 AND LINE 29**, taking the separate $2,000 heat-pump group with it — the one that is easy to miss because it sits outside the named range; pinned by FA-1040-5695-08 and a pipeline test. **(2) THE QM ID NUMBERS (§25C(h))** are a CONDITION of the credit for property placed in service after 2024-12-31 — **TY2025 is the first year it bites and, thanks to the OBBBA termination, the last** — so without them the IRS disallows the item and a 5695 that omits them is not a filable form; 7 PIN facts + `D_5695_QM_PIN`, one finding per category (each number comes from a different manufacturer's certificate); insulation and the audit have no PIN box and never fire. **(3) ONE MAIN HOME ADDRESS**, keyed once and rendered into all four blocks the face asks for it in. **(4) ⚠⚠ THE DOORS ARITHMETIC WAS WRONG:** the face caps the MOST EXPENSIVE door at $250 on its own (19c) BEFORE the $500 aggregate (19h), so v1's `min(30%×all_doors, 500)` **overstated by up to $250 on the commonest doors pattern there is — one replaced $2,000 front door gave $500 where the form gives $250.** **SPEC-FIRST as the rule requires:** RS owns FORM_5695 (⚠ the `5695` lookup 404s — the key is **`FORM_5695`**, which is why the June brief recorded "no spec"); loader amended → integrity gate re-typed and passing → seeded to RS prod → **deployed export verified** → the cached mirror refreshed from that deployed export. Spec 21→46 facts / 4→6 rules / 15→27 lines / 6→12 diagnostics / 9→18 scenarios / 6→8 FA. ⚠ FORM_5695 is the **first form in the RS DB with two version rows**; the lookup is `order_by("-version").first()` so v2 wins, and v1 was set `archived`. ⚠ **ONE PLACE FOR THE CAPS** — the §25C caps had been transcribed THREE times (compute · the renderer summary · `d_5695_25c_cap`, which had inherited the v1 doors bug); they now live once in `credit_25c_parts()`, which render and diagnostics both read. **⚠⚠ AND A GATE THAT WAS NEVER RUNNING: `npx tsc --noEmit` in `client/` compiles NOTHING and exits 0** — the root tsconfig is `{"files": [], "references": [...]}`. The real check is `-p tsconfig.renderer.json`: **86 errors, 34 of them Form 5695** (the `e5695_*` fields were used by the section from the day it shipped but never declared on `TaxpayerData`). Now **52**, all 34 gone, the rest pre-existing. **Also repaired: the RS suite had been RED since `05cbe72`** (a 21st flow assertion against a hardcoded 20, in BOTH test files) → **72 passed**; and `load_assertions()` read spec JSON as cp1252, so an export containing a `§` failed to COLLECT → explicit UTF-8. **LIVE-VERIFIED on the DEMO project** (production never touched): $2,000 on one door → **$250** on screen and on the server (Sch 3 5b) with `l19c`/`l19h` populated · the QM ID box goes RED the moment a cost is keyed without it · 17a=No persists as `false`, zeroes Section A and explains itself · an unanswered gate persists as **`null`**. Scratch values reverted. **Gates: 706 passed** (5695 band + flow **520** + returns + diagnostics) · **vitest 427** (+19) · `seed_rules` AND `seed_form_5695` re-run on BOTH DBs (the new marker rows only appeared after the FormDef re-seed). ✅ **DEPLOY VERIFIED LIVE ON PROD** — bundle `index-CgsV8fRF.js` → **`index-D-t_Kwp7.js`**, carrying four s110-only markers (`Most expensive exterior door` · `QM ID number` · `manufacturer ID number` · `e5695_doors_top`); those strings existed in no earlier build, so the hit proves the new bundle. Server side needed no deploy step — migration + both seeders were run directly against prod. ▶ NEXT: **the 8962 manifest+diagnostics leg** (backlog #12 — ONE authoritative generated-form manifest driving the Forms view, e-file packaging and diagnostics). ⛔ **The 26-return re-triage is CLOSED** — Ken, 2026-07-25: *"let's ignore the triage report."* Do not reopen it or take a build order from `_batch_triage_2026-07-23.md`; `SUPPORTED_FORMS.md` stays the ground truth. 15 of the 26 are entered+verified, 7 remain in Inbox (5 with QA reports explaining the stop), 2 Abney unstarted (1024 has no computed answer key).
