## ▶ PREVIOUS — **s109b (2026-07-25, Ken: "make the spec change and fix the date on 8879"): BOTH REVIEW_QUEUE ITEMS CLOSED.** Commit `cb756a1`; RS `8a30b6c`. **(1) `D_8879_NEED` warning → INFO, SPEC-LED.** Amended at **Rule Studio, which owns this field** — loader amended, harness **79/0**, seeded to RS prod, deployed export verified, then the cached mirror `server/specs/8879_spec.json` refreshed from that export (the ONLY diff vs the old mirror was that one severity; rules/line_map/facts/tests/metadata byte-identical). The rule states a FACT — this return requires an 8879 — that nothing the preparer can do will clear; an unclearable warning is unactionable, and this one did measurable harm (the Batch-001 tester read the surviving finding as evidence the record had been lost and filed a P0 on it, when its presence was proof the record still existed). **Enforcement did NOT move: `D_8879_UNSIGNED` stays the ERROR that blocks transmission**, and both the RS harness and a new app test pin the pair so it can never go all-non-blocking. ⚠ **THE SEVERITY LIVES IN TWO PLACES IN THE APP** — the `_finding()` the rule emits AND the `RULES_8879` registration that seeds the DB rule row the diagnostics catalogue reads; moving only the first would have left the UI filing a finding under one severity while the catalogue advertised another. A test now asserts they agree, and another reads the cached spec mirror so app-vs-spec drift fails a test instead of reaching preparers. `seed_rules` re-run + verified on BOTH DBs; confirmed in the running app via `/api/v1/diagnostic-rules/`. **(2) ONE DATE-ENTRY CONTRACT — WORSE THAN REPORTED.** The 8879/8878 were the app's ONLY raw `<input type="date">` signature fields (every other date uses the shared masked `DateInput`), which is exactly why they took only `2026-07-23` while the preparer date took `07/23/2026`. Swapping them onto the shared control exposed the MIRROR-IMAGE defect in that component: **it silently mangled ISO input — `2026-07-23` displayed as `20/26/0723` and emitted `0723-20-26` (month 20, day 26, year 723) to all 17 of its consumers.** Probed and confirmed BEFORE changing anything. `DateInput` now normalises a whole ISO date arriving in one change (paste/autofill/scripted set) and **NEVER emits a date that does not exist** — month 13 and 31 February yield "" (the same path a half-typed date takes, so nothing is written) with the field flagged `aria-invalid` + red ring, instead of pushing a nonsense date at the server to surface as a generic "Save failed". **Gates:** **vitest 408** (was 396; DateInput 5→12, singletonCardPersistence 7→11) · tsc 0 · `test_8879_8878` 33→**35** · 8879 + flow-assertion bands **560** · core diagnostics bands **109** · RS harness **79/0**. No migrations. **LIVE-VERIFIED ON THE DEMO PROJECT** (production never touched): digits mask as typed (0 → 04 → 04/1 → 04/15 → 04/15/2026) · a pasted `2026-07-23` normalises to `07/23/2026` and PATCHes `{"primary_signed_date":"2026-07-23"}` · `02/31/2025` is flagged and sends NOTHING · correcting it clears the flag and saves; scratch record deleted. ⚠ **RS HARNESS REPAIRED while here:** three of its assertions had failed on EVERY run since 2026-07-15 purely because nobody updated them after Ken approved Gate-1 (they still asserted `READY_TO_SEED` ships False and the FAs are staged DRAFT) — a permanently-red harness is one nobody reads. And the loader printed a hard-coded "flow assertions (staged DRAFT)" on every reseed, which read as though the run had just deactivated the 3 assertions the tts flow gate depends on; **verified against RS prod that all 3 are still `active` (484 active FAs total)** — the message was lying, the behaviour was correct. ⚠⚠ **CARRY THIS: React's blur delegation listens on `focusout`, NOT `blur`.** A synthetic `blur` never reaches `onBlur` in the hidden Browser pane — which is why s108 concluded blur-driven fields were unverifiable there. **They ARE verifiable; dispatch `focusout`.** (A synthetic `focusout` double-fires, so it showed two identical PATCHes — re-checked under RTL, the real path commits exactly once; now pinned, since this codebase has been bitten by double-commit paths before.) ✅ **s109b DEPLOY VERIFIED LIVE, same session:** bundle `index-CdtjNmcu.js` → **`index-CgsV8fRF.js`** — the exact hash the local production build produced — carrying the s109b-only marker `Not a real date` (1 hit against the **0-hit baseline taken BEFORE the push**). The RS severity change went live independently of the bundle (RS serves it from its own DB; both tax-app DBs reseeded directly and verified reading `info`). ▶ **NEXT is unchanged: Form 5695 — input + generated form**, then the 8962 manifest/diagnostics leg.

*(s109 below)*
**s109 (2026-07-25, autonomous "go" — the top unblocked item): FORM 8879 "PERSISTENCE" FIXED, AND THE SERVER WAS RIGHT ALL ALONG.** Commit `edfca9c`, pushed `47002c3..edfca9c`. **CLIENT-ONLY: zero server source changed, no migrations, no production DB writes.** The QA-ranked P0 the external backlog silently dropped: a preparer started an MFJ Form 8879, keyed both PINs and both signature dates, captured the Part I snapshot, opened Forms (the 8879 WAS in the generated package), came back to Input — and the card read **"Start Form 8879"** with every field blank. **NOTHING WAS EVER LOST, AND THE QA REPORT CARRIED ITS OWN PROOF:** the run's final diagnostics still listed `D_8879_NEED`, and `d_8879_need` returns `[]` when the `Form8879` row is absent — so the row demonstrably survived the navigation that appeared to destroy it. The tester read the surviving warning as evidence of the loss; it was evidence of the opposite. **THE ACTUAL DEFECT IS THE SEAM, NOT THE FORM:** a "self-managing" card seeds `useState` from its `initial` prop **once at mount** and thereafter PATCHes the server directly, never telling FormEditor — so `returnData.form_8879` sat at its page-load `null` for the entire session — and the Payments tab is conditionally rendered (`activeTab === "payments" ? <PaymentsSection/>`), so leaving for Forms **unmounts** the card and coming back **remounts** it onto that stale seed. (Clicking "Start Form 8879" again was harmless — the endpoint is `get_or_create`, so the PINs came back — but a preparer seeing a blank card concludes the work is gone, which is exactly what the tester concluded.) **NINE CARDS SHARED THE SHAPE AND ALL NINE ARE FIXED, not just the reported one:** 8879 · 8878 · 4868 · 8888 · 9465 · payment vouchers · 2848 · 3115 · 2553. Each now takes an `onChange` that reports **only what the server confirmed** (a refused PATCH reports nothing, so the parent can never cache a record the DB rejected), and FormEditor's new `setSingleton` records it with an **in-place key write, not a refetch** — none of these cards feeds the 1040 computation from the client, and a refetch would risk stomping unflushed keystrokes elsewhere. **⚠ THE GENERAL LESSON, worth more than the form: a "self-managing" card is only safe while it is MOUNTED.** Anything conditionally rendered must report its server state upward, or its parent's copy becomes a stale seed that silently un-does completed work — and nothing in the existing test suite could see it, because no test ever unmounted and remounted a card mid-session. **Gates:** NEW `singletonCardPersistence.test.tsx` **7** (four of the seven failed before the fix — the reproduction was written first) → **vitest 396** (was 389) · **tsc 0** · NEW `server/tests/test_8879_persistence.py` **7**, which **passed on the FIRST run with no server change** — the cleanest available proof that the DB half was never broken; it pins the hard-reload payload, the **`?fresh_return=1` payload** (that one replaces `returnData` wholesale mid-session, so a missing key there would silently re-stale the seed and reopen the defect), `get_or_create` idempotency, and the diagnostic evidence itself · `test_8879_8878` **33/33** unchanged. **No full server suite run — deliberate** (zero server source changed; the s108e run of 6,192/7/21 stands). **LIVE-VERIFIED ON THE DEMO PROJECT** (`django-demo` → `tts-tax-demo`; production never touched) on John & Judy Jones MFJ 1040 — the same return s94 probed, AGI 35,492: started → Forms → back to Input keeps the card · PINs 54654/16546 + both 07/23/2026 dates + the Part I snapshot survive a **hard reload** AND a second Forms round trip ("within tolerance") · Form 8879 present in the generated package (⚠ it takes ~15s to render — an early check reads as absent) · scratch record deleted, demo left as found. **Two adjacent QA findings filed to `REVIEW_QUEUE.md` rather than changed silently:** (a) **`D_8879_NEED` is a warning no preparer can ever clear** — it fires whenever an 8879 is needed, signed or not, and the actionable gate is the separate `D_8879_UNSIGNED` error; recommend INFO per the s105/s106 D_EIC convention, **not done unilaterally because severity is set by the Rule Studio 8879 spec**, and note this unclearable warning is precisely what misled the tester; (b) one shared **date-entry contract** — the 8879 signature-date fields accept only `2026-07-23` while preparer signature dates accept `07/23/2026`. ✅ **DEPLOY VERIFIED LIVE ON PROD, same session:** bundle `index-XZ-tCoas.js` → **`index-CdtjNmcu.js`** (the same hash the local production build produced) carrying the s109-only marker `onSingletonChange`. The zero-hit baseline was taken BEFORE the push, and a local production build first confirmed the string **survives minification** — so the hit proves the new build, not a pre-existing string. **"Pushed" != "deployed" still stands.** ▶ **NEXT: Form 5695 — input + generated form**, then the 8962 manifest/diagnostics leg (= backlog #12: ONE authoritative generated-form manifest driving Forms view, e-file packaging and diagnostics). **Audit ENTRY vs COMPUTE from an EMPTY form first — and now also ask whether the card survives a TAB SWITCH**; s107 (Schedule D), s108 (1099-R) and s109 (8879) were all entry-layer, all on forms whose compute legs were long green. **⚠ Ken's s106b call is STILL OPEN and deliberately not pre-empted:** the entry agents re-triage the 26-return batch against `D:\tax-test-data\SUPPORTED_FORMS.md` before further engine work.

**s108 (2026-07-25, Ken-directed: the Batch-001 QA backlog, relayed via a ChatGPT-authored 8-workstream prompt): FIVE FIXES SHIPPED — `05bc6a9` `e994354` `3128919` `e3c7168` `c3c4a13` — then (s108e, same day) the test-failure settlement `093f9d9`, **PUSHED `932d80f..807ddc1` and DEPLOY-VERIFIED on prod** once Ken paused entry and gave the go: bundle `index-BkUWdA1A.js` → `index-XZ-tCoas.js` carrying the s108-only marker `"Federal filing status"`, with the zero-hit baseline taken BEFORE the push so the hit proves the new build. Closes the deploy-verification item open since s106e. **⚠⚠ THE EXTERNAL BACKLOG WAS HALF-STALE — audit any externally-authored queue against the code before accepting its order:** Schedule D (s107), 8962 annual mode (s106e), 5695 compute, and the fed→GA sync (s106b) were ALREADY BUILT, and it silently dropped a QA-ranked **P0, Form 8879 persistence**, while keeping lower items. **(1) THE GA RETIREMENT EXCLUSION WAS $0 ON EVERY RETIREE RETURN — AND THE ENGINE WAS RIGHT ALL ALONG.** `GA500_FEDERAL_PULL` carried only two entries (federal AGI, taxable SS), so `compute_ga500.rie()` computed flawlessly over an all-zero base; the v1 comment had deferred the pull as needing "per-spouse allocation judgment." **RULE STUDIO COVERS GEORGIA** (`500`/`GA501`/`GA600S`/`GA700` + AL/NC/SC; live endpoint verified, cache byte-identical, `status: draft` is the house norm) and `R-GA500-RIE` had specced that very judgment since 2026-07-02: *"Each spouse qualifies separately; jointly-owned income split 50/50."* Now pulled per owner, override-respecting: W-2 wages→L1 · materially-participating K-1→L2 earned vs non-mat.-part. + K-1 rental/royalty→L13 · interest/dividends→L6/L7 (joint 50/50) · 1099-R split on the IRA/SEP/SIMPLE box→L11 vs L12 · APPL/65 gates derived from DOB and still overridable. NOT pulled, documented, never zeroed: alimony L8 · capital gains L9 (the per-owner split of the NETTED Schedule D result ≠ the sum of owner-tagged transactions) · other income L10 · Schedule E page-1 rental (`RentalProperty` has no `owner`). `GA_RIE_EARNED_CAP` $5,000 CONFIRMED by Ken and corroborated by O.C.G.A. §48-7-27(a)(5) *controlling* — it was the ONLY UNCITED constant in a block where every neighbour carried a citation, which is the tell worth chasing; citation added, value unchanged. **(2) GA-500 OPENED THE ENTITY EDITOR.** `GA500_SECTION_TABS` starts with `"info"` and `activeTab === "info"` rendered `InfoSection` — legal name, EIN, incorporation, business activity, accounting method, officers — so "Open GA-500" landed preparers on a business-entity screen and the Georgia retirement fields were unreachable. NEW `isIndividualStateForm()` + `StateIndividualInfoSection` for GA-500/SC1040/AL40/NC_D400; GA-501 (fiduciary) and GA-600S/GA-700 (entity) deliberately keep `InfoSection`. `Taxpayer` is OneToOne to the 1040, so a state return has none of its own → NEW read-only `federal_taxpayer` serializer field (read-only ON PURPOSE: a second editable copy of name/SSN is how federal and state identity drift apart). **(3) ⚠ FOUND LIVE, NOT BY ANY TEST: GEORGIA NEVER ATTACHED WITHOUT A W-2.** s106b keyed auto-attach to `W2StateEntry` alone, so a retiree with pension income and no wages got NO Georgia return at all — nothing for the exclusion pull to populate, i.e. the fix could not reach the population it was built for. Now attaches on any GA-tagged W-2/1099-R/1099-INT/1099-DIV (the same sources line 24 aggregates withholding from, so attachment and withholding can no longer disagree); **1099-G deliberately excluded** — it has no state-code box, so treating the practice's file-as-GA convention as a CREATE signal would attach Georgia to every return with a state refund, nonresidents included. Derived RIE lines now also follow their source back DOWN (deleting a 1099-R used to leave a stale exclusion and overstate the refund); safe to always-write because saving a field sets `is_overridden=True`. **(4) ONE 1099-R SPLIT INTO SEVERAL PARTIAL RECORDS.** TWO independent create paths (`handleUpdate` and `handlePayerEinBlur`) with NO in-flight guard on either → a POST per blur, each record holding only the field that created it, with the payer lookup enriching whichever one it happened to make; `+ Add 1099-R` separately POSTed a `"New Payer"` placeholder on every click. Now the s107 draft-card shape: no request on click · nothing POSTs until `payer_name` (a non-blank CharField) · values keyed before it ride the single create · `creatingRef` serialises concurrent blurs onto ONE create · the EIN blur funnels through that same path · **enrichment fills BLANKS ONLY** and never overwrites a typed payer name · inline validation keeps the card and its contents · no mid-entry remount · delete awaits any in-flight create. **(5) AUTOSAVE.** `commit()` bundles every dirty field into one PATCH (s105, deliberate) but DRF rejects the WHOLE payload on one bad field, so an invalid PIN discarded the spouse/address/preparer edits riding with it. Now reads DRF's field-level errors, drops only the named fields and retries once with the remainder; the save result grew `boolean` → `{ok, failed[]}` so rejected fields stay dirty while accepted ones clear. Monotonic `factSeqRef` gives latest-write-wins (a slow FAILED save could paint "Save failed" after a newer save had already succeeded). **⚠ A real fragility surfaced while testing:** `settle()` read `formRef`, which was only assigned during RENDER — an already-resolved save runs its `.then` BEFORE React re-renders, so accepted fields stayed dirty; masked in production only by network latency ordering the render first. The mirror is now updated synchronously. **LIVE-VERIFIED ON THE SEPARATE DEMO SUPABASE PROJECT** (`django-demo` launch config → `tts-tax-demo`; production was NEVER touched at any point), with a scratch MFJ retiree since deleted: GA-500 auto-attached from a 1099-R with no W-2 · the Info tab rendered identity with ZERO entity controls · the worksheet showed spouse L11 521 + L12 44,913 → **L17 45,434** and taxpayer L12 61,497 → L17 61,497 with both gates checked from DOB. **45,434 is the Batch-001 answer-key figure, DERIVED from the governing rule rather than fitted to the target** (TaxWise output is a cross-check, never a source). **Gates:** NEW `test_ga500_rie_federal_pull.py` **20** · NEW `stateIndividualInfo.test.tsx` **13** · NEW `retirement1099RDraftRow.test.tsx` **14** · NEW `autosaveValidationIsolation.test.tsx` **7** → **vitest 389** (was 355) · **tsc 0** · GA-500 band + flow assertions **604 passed**. **NO migrations; no production DB writes.** ✅ **FULL SERVER SUITE CONFIRMED (s108e): 6,192 passed / 7 failed / 21 skipped, 55:25** — the 7 are EXACTLY the known pre-existing set (8915f landing ×2 · AAA-negative ×2 · officer-comp ×2 · manifest-json); +8 over the earlier 6,184 = 7 fixed + 1 new test. **The 7 previously-"unexplained" failures are settled and fixed in `093f9d9`, and they were TWO different problems — bucketing them as one is what hid the real one for two sessions.** (a) `test_user_preferences` ×3 was a **genuinely broken test**: s84 (`c8e9e87`) made `/preparers/` writes ADMIN-only (`IsFirmAdminOrReadOnly`, SEC-1) and the tests, written earlier in `3f70d97`, PATCH as a `Role.PREPARER` — so the endpoint correctly 403s and **the app was right the whole time**; fixture promoted to ADMIN, and the SEC-1 rule (which had **no test at all** — the actual root cause of the two-session mystery) is now pinned by `test_preparer_link_rejects_non_admin`. ⚠ **s106e's "pass in isolation" note about these three was simply WRONG** — they fail in isolation too; nobody re-checked it. (b) `test_seed_backfill_returns` ×4 was real full-suite pollution: ~30 modules seed domain rows from **module-scoped** fixtures via `django_db_blocker.unblock()`, which commits OUTSIDE the per-test transaction, so the rows survive the whole session while these four asserted **global** `TaxReturn.objects.count()`; now firm-scoped. ⚠ **Standing rule: never assert a global row count in a test** — and note a targeted 2-module repro does NOT reproduce this (it depends on fixture instantiation order), so only the full suite proves it. ⚠ Runner hygiene: **Python block-buffers stdout when not a TTY**, so a healthy suite looks dead — use `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1` + `--create-db` and confirm no earlier run is still alive. ⚠ 1099-R and autosave are NOT browser-verified: the Browser pane is hidden, so synthetic focus events do not reach React's `onBlur` (the s105 limitation); their component tests drive the real components via RTL. ⚠ Not unit-tested: the autosave mutation-sequence guard lives inside FormEditor's closure — worth extracting. ▶ NEXT: **Form 8879 persistence (the dropped QA P0). ⚠ DO NOT REBUILD THE FORM — 8879/8878 were built whole in s94 (WO-33: models+migs 0206/0207, Payments-tab cards, `compute_8879_8878` pinned to RS 8879-A..H, `rules_8879` 9 + `rules_8878` 7, f8879 render + signature packet tier, FA-8879-NEED/RESIGN; gate `test_8879_8878` 33/33).** The QA finding is therefore an **entry-layer persistence bug of the s107/s108 class** (placeholder POST · unguarded second create path · blur-commit that never lands) — audit entry-vs-compute first, per the s107 lesson that a green leg in `form_coverage_tracker.md` is NOT proof the leg is usable from an EMPTY form. Reproduce on the DEMO project, never prod. Then Form 5695 input + generated form, then the 8962 manifest/diagnostics leg (= backlog #12: ONE authoritative generated-form manifest driving Forms view, e-file packaging and diagnostics). **⚠ Ken's s106b call is STILL OPEN and deliberately not pre-empted:** the entry agents re-triage the 26-return batch against `D:\tax-test-data\SUPPORTED_FORMS.md` before further engine work — the QA sprint is recorded in `SPRINT_SCOPE.md` but was NOT placed on this Spine, because doing so would answer Ken's question by default.**

## **s107 (2026-07-24, Ken-directed bug fix — the first work inside P1's Schedule D/8949 unit): THE "+ ADD TRANSACTION" BUTTON IS FIXED — Schedule D could not be started at all.** `ScheduleDSection.handleAdd` POSTed `description: ""` the instant the button was clicked; `CapitalTransaction.description` is a non-blank CharField, so DRF answered **400 "This field may not be blank."**, `onRefresh()` re-read an unchanged list, and **no editable row ever appeared** — a dead button on the biggest back-entry unlock (~9 returns). Client-only, ZERO compute code, no migrations, no DB writes. **THE NEW SHAPE:** the click seeds a **client-side draft row** (`SCHD_DRAFT_ID`) held in component state and renders it immediately, fully editable, with NO request; nothing is POSTed until the description is non-blank; columns keyed BEFORE the description accumulate in `draftValuesRef` and are sent WITH it in the single create, so one transaction is always one record. **The duplicate/split guard:** `creatingRef` holds the in-flight create promise, assigned synchronously with the POST's first `await` — any blur landing mid-flight awaits it and PATCHes the id it returns; there is exactly ONE code path that can POST and it is gated on both `draftIdRef` and `creatingRef`, so fast tabbing through six columns can no longer split a lot across two 8949 rows. **Validation is inline** (a note inside the draft card carries the missing-description prompt or the DRF 400 message, `role="alert"`) and the row plus everything typed into it SURVIVE, with the next edit retrying the create. **No mid-entry remount** — once persisted the row keeps rendering AS the draft row (same key, same DOM nodes) with the server row merged in for computed column (h) rather than reappearing as a fresh list row: the s105/s106 lesson that a remount wipes whatever field the preparer had not blurred yet. Delete on an unsaved draft is local-only; on a saved draft it awaits any in-flight create so no record is orphaned. Ordinary-row edit/delete/calculation paths untouched; `ScheduleDSection` exported for testing (the `DueDiligenceAttestation` precedent). **Gates:** NEW `scheduleDDraftRow.test.tsx` **13** (draft appears with zero requests · one record per transaction · pre-description values land on it · gated-promise rapid entry → 1 POST · 400 keeps the row and its values · reload persists · unchanged ordinary-row PATCH path) → **vitest 355** (was 342); NEW `tests/test_schedule_d_draft_row.py` **10** — which pins the API side so the client contract can't drift: the OLD payload still 400s (holding the draft is the only correct behaviour, not a style preference) · one create + PATCHes = 1 row · GET round-trips every column · **gain 3,000 → Sch D line 16 → 1040 line 7, and GA-500 line 8 == 1040 line 11 → Georgia AGI line 10 +3,000** (loss case and PATCH-recompute case too). Flow-assertion gate + Schedule D / 8949 / GA-500 bands re-ran **582 passed**; `tsc` 86 errors before AND after — identical set, all pre-existing. ⚠ **NOT live-verified in a browser**: `server/.env` points dev at the PRODUCTION Supabase project, so a preview click-through writes real `CapitalTransaction` rows to prod; the 13 client tests render the real section and drive real DOM change/blur events instead. ▶ NEXT: **continue P1 — the Schedule D/8949 unit**; the entry layer is now usable, so **audit ENTRY vs COMPUTE for the rest of the form before building** (Topic 9 compute is long-shipped and green — expect more entry-layer gaps like this one, not engine work), then **GA Form 500 RIE** (the 1017 retirement-exclusion mismatch — every retiree). ⚠ **Two deploys await verification**: s106e's 8962 annual method (bundle grep `Line 11 — Annual Calculation`) and s107 (grep the draft-note text `enter a description in column (a)`).

*(s105 below)*
**s105 (2026-07-23, Ken-directed PRIORITY — ADDED to the Spine as S-26, off-spine per the never-silent rule): THE SEVEN ENTRY-LAYER BUGS FROM THE LIVE 1017 BACK-ENTRY PILOT ARE CLOSED (`166e6ef`; mig returns.0209 = a state-only AlterModelOptions; ZERO compute code — flow 518 stands).** **The pilot's headline is that the ENGINE IS RIGHT**: on a real 2025 1040 (wages + interest + two 1099-Rs + Social Security) every line matched TaxWise to the dollar — SS taxability worksheet, the 2025 senior deduction with its MAGI phaseout, the age-65 standard deduction, brackets, withholding aggregation, refund. **Every defect was in the INPUT layer**, and each one is the kind an agent/human fleet mass-produces. **(1) THE 10x AMOUNT-MASK BUG** (Ken: highest priority) — clicking a box showing `0.00` dropped the caret INSIDE the mask, so 25,884 saved as 258,840. NEW `client/src/renderer/lib/selectOnFocus.ts`: ONE delegated `focusin` handler installed at boot selects-all on every amount box (the TaxWise/Lacerte convention) and caps it at 15 chars — a single place to reason about instead of an onFocus prop on ~280 raw inputs, and it covers keyboard/programmatic focus too; a `mouseup` guard re-selects after the click that collapses the caret, and a drag-selection is respected. **(2) CARD LISTS RE-SORTING MID-ENTRY** — the root cause was `Meta.ordering = ["order", <editable name>]` while EVERY card is created with `order=0`, i.e. the list was really sorted ALPHABETICALLY BY PAYER and re-sorted on each keystroke of the name. Adding a 1099-R (the API seeds payer "New Payer") put the new card ABOVE "Teachers Retirement System", so the preparer's next keystrokes landed on the taxpayer's EXISTING card — box 2a became the concatenation 4362511485 and the refund monitor read **$1.68 BILLION**. The tie-break is now the immutable `created_at` on **all eight** document-card models (W-2 · 1099-INT · 1099-DIV · 1099-R · Sch C · Sch C other-expense · Sch F · Sch F other-expense); cards append and STAY PUT. **(3) NUMERIC OVERFLOW → 500** — the columns are wide enough (`max_digits=15`) to STORE the garbage, so DRF's own check never fired and the value reached the compute chain. NEW `MoneyBoundedSerializer` (1040 entry cards + taxpayer facts ONLY — business figures legitimately run to billions) returns **400** at ≥$1,000,000,000 with a per-field message; `api.ts` now NAMES the field in the failed-save banner. **(4) THE FILING-STATUS WIPE** — these cards commit on BLUR but a `<select>` commits on CHANGE, and that save re-seeds every card from the server, so anything typed-but-not-yet-blurred was gone from the screen AND had never reached the DB. `useTaxpayerFacts` now keeps dirty fields across a re-seed AND flushes them alongside whatever is being committed. **(5) DOB** — commits as soon as the date is COMPLETE (not only on blur) + NEW **`D_1040_018`** warning on a missing taxpayer/spouse DOB: on the pilot, blank DOBs silently dropped **$15,200** of age-derived deductions (tax over by $1,824) with no diagnostic at all. **(6) DIAGNOSTIC SIGNAL** — `D_EIC_007` is now **INFO** when the engine auto-resolved it from the DOB (the childless age test), so retiree-with-wages returns stop opening with an unactionable red; it stays an ERROR when the preparer ASSERTED claimed-as-dependent / QC-of-another. `LATE_FILING` is **suppressed** for `rolled_from_source='backfill-2025'` — all ~3,300 shells are late by design. **(7) THE SEEDED-SHELL BANNER** now reads "Seeded for 2025 back-entry", not the false "carried values were copied from the prior-year return"; `BACKFILL_RETURN_SOURCE` is a shared constant on both sides. **PLUS (8) the NEW DEFAULT "Tax Shelter" FOREST THEME** (Ken: "more forest green, less cream") — forest `#1e362a` carries the structure (nav, primary, ink), the canvas moves off cream onto pale green, gold `#a9884f` is a garnish, Georgia serif headings; sampled from the firm's own work in `D:\dev\taxshelter-design`. **Ledger stays in the list unchanged as the one-click rollback**, and the RED/YELLOW/GREEN data-entry convention is untouched as always. Gates: flow **518** · NEW `test_entry_layer_s105` **10** (incl. the no-editable-name-in-Meta.ordering DRIFT GUARD) · NEW `test_entry_layer_diagnostics_s105` **11** · returns/start-return/topic3/5/7/spine/diagnostics bands **267** · spine rule-count pin re-cut **17→18** · tsc 0 · **vitest 325** (was 300) · BOTH DBs migrated + `seed_rules` re-run. **🏁 LIVE VERIFICATION ON THE PILOT RETURN ITSELF** (opened, never re-created): banner correct · 2 cards in stable order with values intact · `focusin` on a `0.00` box selects [0,4] + caps maxLength 15 while the payer-name field is untouched · `PATCH taxable_amount=4362511485` → **400** with the field message, card unchanged at 43,625.00 · **diagnostics went from 5 findings (1 ERROR + 3 warnings) to 2, both INFO**. clients 3675 / entities 3979 unchanged; throwaway probe user removed. ▶ NEXT: **Render must deploy `166e6ef`** before Ken re-tests on prep.delviotax.com; then **the fleet back-entry can run**. **S-18 · [APP] ∥ · PLATFORM PRE-LAUNCH TRIO (added 2026-07-09, Ken-directed — ideas fleshed
out in Claude-chat; prompts in hand for 18a-c).** Non-tax platform work — no compute risk;

system is a reason this app exists (18a); PWA desktop install à la QBO = big value add (18c).
- [ ] **18a · Optimistic concurrency on returns** — version column on the return record;
  DRF save-time version check (reject stale saves with 409); React "modified elsewhere —
  reload" prompt. **NO pessimistic locks anywhere** — reentry must never depend on cleanup
  by a crashed process (the TaxWise failure mode). ⚠ Build-time design detail: version
  granularity vs the per-field focusout-autosave cadence (FormFieldValue) — a preparer must
  not 409 against their own parallel autosaves; composes with the s35 `select_for_update`
  row locks (same-row write serialization) — versioning adds the cross-session staleness
  guard. Priority: pre-January; Ken's roadmap slots it after client numbering wraps.

  with self-expiring TTL (~60s). Warns, never blocks. Fast follow to 18a.
